So if we now pass to the limit on both sides of (
A.5
) it follows that

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(A^{\prime\prime}\right)_{t}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}\right)_{t}dt\right]={\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(A^{\prime\prime}\right)_{t}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}\right)_{t}dt\right].

(A.16)

Therefore

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(A^{\prime}\right)_{t}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}\right)_{t}dt\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(A^{\prime}-A^{\prime\prime}\right)_{t}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}\right)_{t}dt\right]+{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(A^{\prime\prime}\right)_{t}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}\right)_{t}dt\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(A^{\prime}-A^{\prime\prime}\right)_{t}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}\right)_{t}dt\right]+{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(A^{\prime\prime}\right)_{t}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}\right)_{t}dt\right].

(A.17)

Similarly to (
A.5
),

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(\epsilon^{A^{\prime\prime},n}\right)_{t}^{2}dt\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{N_{n}}\sum_{j_{2}=1}^{N_{n}}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(w_{j_{1}}^{n}\right)_{t}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(w_{j_{2}}^{n}\right)_{t}dt{\mathbb{P}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left(\left\{\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}\in Q_{j_{1}}^{n}\right\}\cap\left\{\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}\in Q_{j_{2}}^{n}\right\}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{N_{n}}\sum_{j_{2}=1}^{N_{n}}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(w_{j_{1}}^{n}\right)_{t}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(w_{j_{2}}^{n}\right)_{t}dt{\mathbb{P}}_{A^{\prime}}\left(\epsilon_{A^{\prime}}\in Q_{j_{1}}^{n}\cap Q_{j_{2}}^{n}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{N_{n}}\sum_{j_{2}=1}^{N_{n}}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(w_{j_{1}}^{n}\right)_{t}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(w_{j_{2}}^{n}\right)_{t}dt{\mathbb{P}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left(\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}\in Q_{j_{1}}^{n}\cap Q_{j_{2}}^{n}\right)

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(\epsilon^{A^{\prime},n}\right)_{t}^{2}dt\right].

(A.18)

By passing to the limit we obtain

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}\right)_{t}^{2}dt\right]={\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(\epsilon^{A^{\prime\prime}}\right)_{t}^{2}dt\right].

(A.19)

Returning to the first term of (
A.5
),

\displaystyle\left|{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(Y^{A^{\prime}}_{t}\right)^{2}dt\right]-{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(Y^{A^{\prime\prime}}_{t}dt\right)^{2}\right]\right|

\displaystyle=\left|{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(A^{\prime}+\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}\right)_{t}^{2}dt\right]-{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(A^{\prime\prime}+\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}\right)_{t}^{2}dt\right]\right|

\displaystyle=\left|{\mathbb{E}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(A^{\prime}\right)_{t}^{2}-\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(A^{\prime\prime}\right)_{t}^{2}\right)dt\right]+2{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(A^{\prime}\right)_{t}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}\right)_{t}dt\right]-2{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(A^{\prime\prime}\right)_{t}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}\right)_{t}dt\right]\right.

\displaystyle\left.+{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}\right)_{t}^{2}dt\right]-{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}\right)_{t}^{2}dt\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(A^{\prime}+A^{\prime\prime}\right)_{t}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(A^{\prime}-A^{\prime\prime}\right)_{t}dt\right|\right]+{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\left|\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(A^{\prime}-A^{\prime\prime}\right)_{t}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}\right)_{t}dt\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(A^{\prime}+A^{\prime\prime}\right)_{t}\right)^{2}dt\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(A^{\prime}-A^{\prime\prime}\right)_{t}\right)^{2}dt\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right]

\displaystyle+{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(A^{\prime}-A^{\prime\prime}\right)_{t}^{2}dt\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}\right)_{t}^{2}dt\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[\lVert\mathcal{S}_{1}(A^{\prime}+A^{\prime\prime})\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p+1}}\lVert\mathcal{S}_{1}(A^{\prime}-A^{\prime\prime})\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p+1}}\right]+{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\lVert\mathcal{S}_{1}(A^{\prime}-A^{\prime\prime})\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p+1}}\lVert\mathcal{S}_{1}(\epsilon^{A^{\prime}})\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p+1}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\lVert\mathcal{S}\rVert^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\lVert A^{\prime\prime}-A^{\prime}\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p+1}}^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}\left({\mathbb{E}}\left[\lVert A^{\prime\prime}+A^{\prime}\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p+1}}^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}+{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\lVert\mathcal{S}_{1}(\epsilon^{A^{\prime}})\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p+1}}^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2\lVert\mathcal{S}\rVert^{2}\lVert A^{\prime\prime}-A^{\prime}\rVert_{\mathcal{V}}\left(\lVert A^{\prime}\rVert_{\mathcal{V}}+\lVert A^{\prime\prime}\rVert_{\mathcal{V}}+\lVert\epsilon\rVert_{\mathcal{V}}\right),

where we applied the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality several times and utilized both (
A.5
) and (
A.19
) to cancel out terms.

Term 2 of
(
A.5
)

For the second term on the right-hand side of (
A.5
) we first make the following expansion,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}Y^{A^{\prime}}_{t}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)X^{A^{\prime}}_{\tau}(i)d\tau dt\right]-{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}Y^{A^{\prime\prime}}_{t}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)X^{A^{\prime\prime}}_{\tau}(i)d\tau dt\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(A^{\prime}+\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}\right)_{t}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(A^{\prime}+\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}\right)_{\tau}d\tau dt\right]

\displaystyle-{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(A^{\prime\prime}+\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}\right)_{t}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(A^{\prime\prime}+\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}\right)_{\tau}d\tau dt\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(A^{\prime}-A^{\prime\prime}\right)_{t}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(A^{\prime}\right)_{\tau}d\tau dt\right]

\displaystyle+{\mathbb{E}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(A^{\prime\prime}\right)_{t}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(A^{\prime}-A^{\prime\prime}\right)_{\tau}d\tau dt\right]

\displaystyle+{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}\right)_{t}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}\right)_{\tau}d\tau dt\right]-{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}\right)_{t}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}\right)_{\tau}d\tau dt\right]

\displaystyle+{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(A^{\prime}\right)_{t}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}\right)_{\tau}d\tau dt\right]-{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(A^{\prime\prime}\right)_{t}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}\right)_{\tau}d\tau dt\right]

\displaystyle+{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}\right)_{t}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(A^{\prime}\right)_{\tau}d\tau dt\right]-{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}\right)_{t}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(A^{\prime\prime}\right)_{\tau}d\tau dt\right]

(A.20)

We bound the first term on the right-most side above

\displaystyle\left|{\mathbb{E}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(A^{\prime}-A^{\prime\prime}\right)_{t}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(A^{\prime}\right)_{\tau}d\tau dt\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]^{2}}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(A^{\prime}-A^{\prime\prime}\right)_{t}^{2}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(A^{\prime}\right)_{\tau}^{2}d\tau dt\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(\beta(t,\tau))^{2}(i)d\tau dt\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(A^{\prime}-A^{\prime\prime}\right)_{t}^{2}dt\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(A^{\prime}\right)_{\tau}^{2}d\tau\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]^{2}}(\beta(t,\tau))^{2}(i)d\tau dt\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[\lVert\mathcal{S}_{1}(A^{\prime}-A^{\prime\prime})\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]}\lVert\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(A^{\prime}\right)\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]}\right]\lVert\beta(i)\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}

\displaystyle\leq\lVert\beta(i)\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}\lVert\mathcal{S}\rVert^{2}\lVert A^{\prime}-A^{\prime\prime}\rVert_{\mathcal{V}}\lVert A^{\prime}\rVert_{\mathcal{V}}

(A.21)

analogously for the second term we have

\displaystyle\left|{\mathbb{E}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(A^{\prime\prime}\right)_{t}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(A^{\prime}-A^{\prime\prime}\right)_{\tau}d\tau dt\right]\right|\leq\lVert\beta(i)\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}\lVert\mathcal{S}\rVert^{2}\lVert A^{\prime}-A^{\prime\prime}\rVert_{\mathcal{V}}\lVert A^{\prime\prime}\rVert_{\mathcal{V}}.

For terms three and four of (
A.5
), analogously to (
A.5
),

\displaystyle\left|{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(\epsilon^{A^{\prime},n}-\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}\right)_{t}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(\epsilon^{A^{\prime},n}-\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}\right)_{\tau}d\tau dt\right]\right|\leq\lVert\beta(i)\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}\lVert\mathcal{S}\rVert^{2}\lVert\epsilon^{n}-\epsilon\rVert_{\mathcal{V}}^{2},

which converges to zero, implying

\displaystyle\lim_{n\to\infty}

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(\epsilon^{A^{\prime},n}-\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}\right)_{t}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(\epsilon^{A^{\prime},n}-\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}\right)_{\tau}d\tau dt\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}\right)_{t}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}\right)_{\tau}d\tau dt\right].

Meanwhile, arguing as in (
A.5
) shows that

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(\epsilon^{A^{\prime},n}\right)_{t}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(\epsilon^{A^{\prime},n}\right)_{\tau}d\tau dt\right]={\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}^{n}\right)_{t}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}^{n}\right)_{\tau}d\tau dt\right].

Similarly to (
A.16
) this leads to

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}\right)_{t}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}\right)_{\tau}d\tau dt\right]={\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}\right)_{t}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}\right)_{\tau}d\tau dt\right],

hence terms three and four of (
A.5
) cancel. Terms five and six of (
A.5
) are handled with similar techniques,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(A^{\prime}\right)_{t}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}\right)_{\tau}d\tau dt\right]={\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(A^{\prime}\right)_{t}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}\right)_{\tau}d\tau dt\right]

implying

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(A^{\prime}\right)_{t}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}\right)_{\tau}d\tau dt\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(A^{\prime}-A^{\prime\prime}\right)_{t}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}\right)_{\tau}d\tau dt\right]

\displaystyle+{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(A^{\prime\prime}\right)_{t}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}\right)_{\tau}d\tau dt\right].

Similarly for terms seven and eight of (
A.5
),

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}\right)_{t}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(A^{\prime}\right)_{\tau}d\tau dt\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}\right)_{t}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(A^{\prime}-A^{\prime\prime}\right)_{\tau}d\tau dt\right]

\displaystyle+{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}\right)_{t}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(A^{\prime\prime}\right)_{\tau}d\tau dt\right]

This leads to

\displaystyle\left|{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}Y^{A^{\prime}}_{t}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)X^{A^{\prime}}_{\tau}(i)d\tau dt\right]-{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}Y^{A^{\prime\prime}}_{t}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)X^{A^{\prime\prime}}_{\tau}(i)d\tau dt\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|{\mathbb{E}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(A^{\prime}-A^{\prime\prime}\right)_{t}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(A^{\prime}\right)_{\tau}d\tau dt\right]\right|

\displaystyle+\left|{\mathbb{E}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(A^{\prime\prime}\right)_{t}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(A^{\prime}-A^{\prime\prime}\right)_{\tau}d\tau dt\right]\right|

\displaystyle+\left|{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}\right)_{t}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(A^{\prime}-A^{\prime\prime}\right)_{\tau}d\tau dt\right]\right|

\displaystyle+\left|{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(A^{\prime}-A^{\prime\prime}\right)_{t}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}\right)_{\tau}d\tau dt\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\lVert\beta(i)\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}\lVert\mathcal{S}\rVert^{2}\lVert A^{\prime}-A^{\prime\prime}\rVert_{\mathcal{V}}\left(\lVert A^{\prime}\rVert_{\mathcal{V}}+\lVert A^{\prime\prime}\rVert_{\mathcal{V}}\right)+2\lVert\beta(i)\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}\lVert\mathcal{S}\rVert^{2}\lVert A^{\prime}-A^{\prime\prime}\rVert_{\mathcal{V}}\lVert\epsilon\rVert_{\mathcal{V}}.

Term 3 of
(
A.5
)

We expand the third term of (
A.5
),

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)X^{A^{\prime}}_{\tau}(i)d\tau\right)^{2}dt\right]-{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)X^{A^{\prime\prime}}_{\tau}(i)d\tau\right)^{2}dt\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)S_{i+1}\left(A^{\prime}+\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}\right)_{\tau}(i)d\tau\right)^{2}dt\right]

\displaystyle-{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)S_{i+1}\left(A^{\prime\prime}+\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}\right)_{\tau}(i)d\tau\right)^{2}dt\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(A^{\prime\prime}\right)_{\tau}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)d\tau\right)^{2}dt\right]-{\mathbb{E}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(A^{\prime}\right)_{\tau}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)d\tau\right)^{2}dt\right]

\displaystyle+{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}\right)_{\tau}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)d\tau\right)^{2}dt\right]-{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}\right)_{\tau}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)d\tau\right)^{2}dt\right]

\displaystyle+{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}\right)_{\tau}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)d\tau\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(A^{\prime}\right)_{\tau}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)d\tau dt\right]

\displaystyle-{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}\right)_{\tau}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)d\tau\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(A^{\prime\prime}\right)_{\tau}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)d\tau dt\right].

(A.22)

For the first term difference the right-hand side of (
A.5
), note that

\displaystyle\left|{\mathbb{E}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(A^{\prime\prime}\right)_{\tau}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)d\tau\right)^{2}dt\right]-{\mathbb{E}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(A^{\prime}\right)_{\tau}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)d\tau\right)^{2}dt\right]\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left|{\mathbb{E}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(A^{\prime\prime}-A^{\prime\prime}\right)_{\tau}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)d\tau\right)\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(A^{\prime\prime}+A^{\prime\prime}\right)_{\tau}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)d\tau\right)dt\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(A^{\prime\prime}-A^{\prime\prime}\right)_{\tau}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)d\tau\right)^{2}dt\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(A^{\prime\prime}+A^{\prime\prime}\right)_{\tau}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)d\tau\right)^{2}dt\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(A^{\prime\prime}-A^{\prime\prime}\right)_{\tau}^{2}d\tau\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(\beta(t,\tau))^{2}(i)d\tau dt\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(A^{\prime\prime}-A^{\prime\prime}\right)_{\tau}^{2}d\tau\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(\beta(t,\tau))^{2}(i)d\tau dt\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(A^{\prime\prime}-A^{\prime\prime}\right)_{\tau}^{2}d\tau\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(\beta(t,\tau))^{2}(i)d\tau\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}dt\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle=\lVert\beta(i)\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\lVert\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(A^{\prime\prime}-A^{\prime\prime}\right)\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}\lVert\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(A^{\prime\prime}+A^{\prime\prime}\right)\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\lVert\beta(i)\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\lVert\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(A^{\prime\prime}-A^{\prime\prime}\right)\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\lVert\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(A^{\prime\prime}+A^{\prime\prime}\right)\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\lVert\beta(i)\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}^{2}\lVert\mathcal{S}\rVert^{2}\lVert A^{\prime\prime}-A^{\prime}\rVert_{\mathcal{V}}\left(\lVert A^{\prime\prime}\rVert_{\mathcal{V}}+\lVert A^{\prime}\rVert_{\mathcal{V}}\right).

For the second difference on the right-hand side of (
A.5
),

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}^{n}-\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}\right)_{\tau}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)d\tau\right)^{2}dt\right]

\displaystyle\leq\lVert\beta(i)\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\lVert\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}^{n}-\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}\right)\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\lVert\beta(i)\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\lVert\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}^{n}-\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}\right)\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\lVert\beta(i)\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}^{2}\lVert\mathcal{S}\rVert^{2}\lVert\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}^{n}-\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}\rVert_{\mathcal{V}}^{2},

which converges to zero, implying that

\lim_{n\to\infty}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}^{n}\right)_{\tau}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)d\tau\right)^{2}dt\right]={\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}\right)_{\tau}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)d\tau\right)^{2}dt\right]

and analogously

\lim_{n\to\infty}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}^{n}\right)_{\tau}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)d\tau\right)^{2}dt\right]={\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}\right)_{\tau}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)d\tau\right)^{2}dt\right].

Meanwhile, analogously to (
A.5
),

{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}^{n}\right)_{\tau}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)d\tau\right)^{2}dt\right]={\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(\epsilon^{A^{\prime},n}\right)_{\tau}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)d\tau\right)^{2}dt\right],

which therefore leads to

{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}\right)_{\tau}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)d\tau\right)^{2}dt\right]={\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}\right)_{\tau}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)d\tau\right)^{2}dt\right].

Hence the second difference in (
A.5
) is cancelled.
For the third difference in (
A.5
),

\displaystyle\left|{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}-\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}^{n}\right)_{\tau}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)d\tau\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(A^{\prime\prime}\right)_{\tau}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)d\tau dt\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(\beta(t,\tau))^{2}(i)d\tau dt{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}-\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}^{n}\right)_{\tau}^{2}d\tau\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(A^{\prime\prime}\right)_{\tau}^{2}d\tau\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right]

\displaystyle=\lVert\beta(i)\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\lVert\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}-\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}^{n}\right)\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}\lVert\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(A^{\prime\prime}\right)\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\lVert\beta(i)\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\lVert\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}-\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}^{n}\right)\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\lVert\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(A^{\prime\prime}\right)\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\lVert\beta(i)\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}^{2}\lVert S\rVert^{2}\lVert\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}-\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}^{n}\rVert_{\mathcal{V}}\lVert A^{\prime\prime}\rVert_{\mathcal{V}},

which converges to zero, implying that

\displaystyle\lim_{n\to\infty}

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}^{n}\right)_{\tau}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)d\tau\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(A^{\prime\prime}\right)_{\tau}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)d\tau dt\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}\right)_{\tau}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)d\tau\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(A^{\prime\prime}\right)_{\tau}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)d\tau dt\right]

and analogously

\displaystyle\lim_{n\to\infty}

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(\epsilon^{A^{\prime},n}\right)_{\tau}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)d\tau\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(A^{\prime}\right)_{\tau}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)d\tau dt\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}\right)_{\tau}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)d\tau\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(A^{\prime}\right)_{\tau}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)d\tau dt\right]

Meanwhile

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(\epsilon^{A^{\prime},n}\right)_{\tau}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)d\tau\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(A^{\prime}\right)_{\tau}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)d\tau dt\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}^{n}\right)_{\tau}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)d\tau\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(A^{\prime}\right)_{\tau}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)d\tau dt\right]

which therefore leads to

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}\right)_{\tau}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)d\tau\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(A^{\prime}\right)_{\tau}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)d\tau dt\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}\right)_{\tau}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)d\tau\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(A^{\prime}\right)_{\tau}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)d\tau dt\right].

This implies,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}\right)_{\tau}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)d\tau\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(A^{\prime}\right)_{\tau}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)d\tau dt\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}\right)_{\tau}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)d\tau\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(A^{\prime}-A^{\prime\prime}\right)_{\tau}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)d\tau dt\right]

\displaystyle+{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}\right)_{\tau}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)d\tau\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(A^{\prime\prime}\right)_{\tau}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)d\tau dt\right],

(A.23)

which allows us to compute the following bound for the third difference in (
A.5
),

\displaystyle\left|{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}\right)_{\tau}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)d\tau\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(A^{\prime}\right)_{\tau}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)d\tau dt\right]\right.

\displaystyle\left.-{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}\right)_{\tau}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)d\tau\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(A^{\prime\prime}\right)_{\tau}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)d\tau dt\right]\right|

\displaystyle=\left|{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}\right)_{\tau}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)d\tau\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{i+1}\left(A^{\prime}-A^{\prime\prime}\right)_{\tau}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)d\tau dt\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\lVert\beta(i)\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}^{2}\lVert\mathcal{S}\rVert^{2}\lVert A^{\prime\prime}-A^{\prime}\rVert_{\mathcal{V}}\lVert\epsilon\rVert_{\mathcal{V}}.

(A.24)

We may now bound the third term of (
A.5
),

\displaystyle\left|{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)X^{A^{\prime}}_{\tau}(i)d\tau\right)^{2}dt\right]-{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)X^{A^{\prime\prime}}_{\tau}(i)d\tau\right)^{2}dt\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\lVert\beta(i)\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}^{2}\lVert\mathcal{S}\rVert^{2}\lVert A^{\prime\prime}-A^{\prime}\rVert_{\mathcal{V}}\left(\lVert A^{\prime\prime}\rVert_{\mathcal{V}}+\lVert A^{\prime}\rVert_{\mathcal{V}}+\lVert\epsilon\rVert_{\mathcal{V}}\right)

It now follows that there exists some
E\in{\mathbb{R}}^{+}
(depending on
\lVert\beta\rVert^{2}_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})^{p}}
and
\lVert\mathcal{S}\rVert^{2}
) such that

\displaystyle\left|R_{A^{\prime}}(\beta)-R_{A^{\prime\prime}}(\beta)\right|\leq E\left(\lVert A^{\prime}\rVert_{\mathcal{V}}+\lVert A^{\prime\prime}\rVert_{\mathcal{V}}+\lVert\epsilon\rVert_{\mathcal{V}}\right)\lVert A^{\prime}-A^{\prime\prime}\rVert_{\mathcal{V}}

(A.25)

from which the result follows.
∎

We now proceed with the proof of the main Theorem.

Proof of Theorem
3.7
.

Step 1: Establish regularity/summability properties for the target and the covariates and expand the risk function in terms of scores.

Take
A^{\prime}\in\mathcal{V}
, clearly,

(Y^{A^{\prime}},X^{A^{\prime}})=\mathcal{S}\left({A^{\prime}}+\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}\right).

Therefore,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(Y_{t}^{A^{\prime}})^{2}dt\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(Y_{t}^{A^{\prime}})^{2}dt\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\pi_{1}\left(\mathcal{S}\left(A^{\prime}+\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}\right)\right)^{2}_{t}dt\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\|\left(\pi_{1}\left(\mathcal{S}\left(A^{\prime}+\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}\right)\right),0\ldots,0\right)\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p+1}}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathcal{S}\|^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\|A^{\prime}+\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p+1}}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathcal{S}\|^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[2\|A^{\prime}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p+1}}^{2}+2\|\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p+1}}^{2}\right]<\infty,

which also implies
Y^{A^{\prime}}\in L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])
a.s.. Analogously we have that
{\mathbb{E}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(X_{t}(i)^{A^{\prime}})^{2}dt\right]<\infty
and
X^{A^{\prime}}(i)\in L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])
a.s., for
1\leq i\leq p
. Consider
\mathcal{K}_{X^{A^{\prime}}(i)}:L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])\to L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])
, defined by

(\mathcal{K}_{X^{A^{\prime}}(i)}f)(t)=\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}K_{X^{A^{\prime}}(i)}(s,t)f(s)ds,

for
f\in L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])
. By the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality

\displaystyle\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]^{2}}K_{X^{A^{\prime}}(i)}(s,t)^{2}dsdt

\displaystyle=\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[X^{A^{\prime}}_{s}(i)X^{A^{\prime}}_{t}(i)\right]^{2}dsdt

\displaystyle\leq\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\left(X_{s}^{A^{\prime}}(i)\right)^{2}\right]{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\left(X_{t}^{A^{\prime}}(j)\right)^{2}\right]dsdt

\displaystyle=\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\left(X_{t}^{A^{\prime}}(i)\right)^{2}\right]dt\right)^{2},

which is finite by the assumption on
A
and
\epsilon
. It follows that
\mathcal{K}_{X^{A^{\prime}}(i)}
induces a compact self-adjoint operator on
L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p}
and therefore, by the Hilbert-Schmidt theorem,
\mathcal{K}_{X^{A^{\prime}}(i)}
has an eigendecomposition,
\mathcal{K}_{X^{A^{\prime}}(i)}f=\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\alpha_{i,k}\langle\psi_{i,k},f\rangle\psi_{i,k}
, for
f\in L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])
, where its eigenfunctions,
\{\psi_{i,k}\}_{k\in{\mathbb{N}}}
, are orthonormal in
L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])
and the series converges in
L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])
. If
\mathsf{Ker}\left(\mathcal{K}_{X^{A^{\prime}}(i)}\right)\not=\{0\}
then there exists an ON-basis,
\{\eta_{i,l}\}_{l\in{\mathbb{N}}}
for
\mathsf{Ker}\left(\mathcal{K}_{X^{A^{\prime}}}\right)
. Let
\mathcal{L}_{1}=\overline{\mathsf{span}}\left(\{\psi_{i,k}\}_{k\in{\mathbb{N}}}\right)
and
\mathcal{L}_{2}:=\mathcal{L}_{1}^{\perp}=\mathsf{Ker}\left(\mathcal{K}_{X^{A^{\prime}}}\right)
. Then
\mathcal{L}_{2}
is a closed subspace of
L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])
and is therefore separable, which implies it has a countable ON-basis,
\{\eta_{k}\}_{k}
and moreover
L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])=\mathcal{L}_{1}\oplus\mathcal{L}_{2}
as well as
L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])=\overline{\mathsf{span}}\left(\{\psi_{i,k}\}_{k}\cup\{\eta_{i,k}\}_{k}\right)
. Let
\{\tilde{\psi}_{i,k}\}_{k}
be an enumeration of the ON-system
\{\psi_{i,k}\}_{k\in{\mathbb{N}}}\cup\{\eta_{i,k}\}_{k\in{\mathbb{N}}}
, which is a basis for
L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])
. Take some arbitrary ON-basis of
L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])
,
\{\phi_{n}\}_{n\in{\mathbb{N}}}
and define

•

Z^{A^{\prime}}_{k}=\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}Y^{A^{\prime}}_{t}\phi_{k}(t)dt
and

•

\chi^{A^{\prime}}_{k}(i)=\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}X^{A^{\prime}}_{t}(i)\tilde{\psi}_{i,k}(t)dt
.

Then it follows that
X^{A^{\prime}}(i)\in L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])
a.s. and hence if we let
S_{n}^{i*}(t)=\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(\chi^{(1),A^{\prime}}_{k}(i)\psi_{i,k}(t)+\chi^{(2),A^{\prime}}_{k}(i)\eta_{i,k}(t)\right)
then
S_{n}^{i*}\xrightarrow{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}X^{A^{\prime}}(i)
a.s. (since
\{\tilde{\psi}_{i,k}\}_{k}
is an ON-basis for
L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])
). Moreover, note that

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\left(\chi^{(2),A^{\prime}}_{l}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{0}^{T}\int_{0}^{T}\eta_{i,l}(s)X^{A^{\prime}}_{s}(i)X^{A^{\prime}}_{t}(i)\eta_{i,l}(t)dtds\right]

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{T}\int_{0}^{T}\eta_{i,l}(s){\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[X_{s}^{A^{\prime}}(i)X_{t}^{A^{\prime}}(i)\right]\eta_{i,l}(t)dtds

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{T}\int_{0}^{T}\eta_{i,l}(s)K_{X^{A^{\prime}}(i)}(t,s)\eta_{i,l}(t)dtds

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{T}(\mathcal{K}_{X^{A^{\prime}}(i)}\eta_{i,l})(t)\eta_{i,l}(t)^{T}dtds

\displaystyle=\lim_{N\to\infty}\int_{0}^{T}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\alpha_{i,n}\langle\psi_{i,n},\eta_{i,l}\rangle_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}\psi_{i,n}(t)\eta_{i,l}(t)dt

\displaystyle=\lim_{N\to\infty}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\alpha_{i,n}\langle\psi_{i,n},\eta_{i,l}\rangle_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}\int_{0}^{T}\psi_{i,n}(t)\eta_{i,l}(t)dt=0.

This implies that if we let
S_{n}^{X^{A^{\prime}}(i)}(t)=\sum_{k=1}^{n}\chi^{(1),A^{\prime}}_{k}(i)\psi_{i,k}(t)
, then
S_{n}^{X^{A^{\prime}}(i)}\xrightarrow{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}X^{A^{\prime}}(i)
a.s.. We will therefore denote
\chi_{l}^{A^{\prime}}(i)=\chi^{(1),A^{\prime}}_{l}(i)
from now on. Since we expand
X^{A^{\prime}}(i)
in the basis given by the eigenfunctions of
K_{X^{A^{\prime}}(i)}
we also have that the sequence
\{\chi_{l}^{A^{\prime}}(i)\}_{l\in{\mathbb{N}}}
is orthogonal,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\chi_{l_{1}}^{A^{\prime}}(i)\chi_{l_{2}}^{A^{\prime}}(i)\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}X^{A^{\prime}}_{s}(i)\psi_{i,l_{1}}(s)ds\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}X^{A^{\prime}}_{t}(i)\psi_{i,l_{2}}(t)dt\right]

\displaystyle=\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\psi_{l_{1}}(s){\mathbb{E}}\left[X^{A^{\prime}}_{s}(i)X^{A^{\prime}}_{t}(i)\right]\psi_{l_{2}}(t)dtds

\displaystyle=\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\psi_{i,l_{1}}(s)K_{X^{A^{\prime}}(i)}(t,s)\psi_{i,l_{2}}(t)dtds

\displaystyle=\lim_{N\to\infty}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\alpha_{n}\langle\psi_{i,n},\psi_{i,l_{1}}\rangle_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}\psi_{i,n}(t)\psi_{i,l_{2}}(t)dtds

\displaystyle=\lim_{N\to\infty}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\alpha_{n}\langle\psi_{i,n},\psi_{i,l_{1}}\rangle_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}\langle\psi_{i,n},\psi_{i,l_{2}}\rangle_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}

\displaystyle=\lim_{N\to\infty}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\alpha_{n}\delta_{n,l_{1}}\delta_{n,l_{2}}=\alpha_{l_{1}}\delta_{l_{1},l_{2}}.

(A.26)

If also let
S_{n}^{Y^{A^{\prime}}}(t)=\sum_{k=1}^{n}Z^{A^{\prime}}_{k}\phi_{k}(t)
then, since
\{\phi_{k}\}_{k\in{\mathbb{N}}}
is an ON-basis,
S_{n}^{Y^{A^{\prime}}}\xrightarrow{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}Y^{A^{\prime}}
and
S_{n}^{X^{A^{\prime}}(i)}\xrightarrow{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}X^{A^{\prime}}(i)
. Next, by monotone convergence

\displaystyle\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[(X^{A^{\prime}}_{t}(i))^{2}\right]dt

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(X^{A^{\prime}}_{t}(i))^{2}dt\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\lim_{n\to\infty}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{n}\chi^{A^{\prime}}_{k}(i)\psi_{i,k}(t)\right)^{2}dt\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\lim_{n\to\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\chi^{A^{\prime}}_{k}(i)\chi^{A^{\prime}}_{l}(i)\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\psi_{i,l}(t)\psi_{i,k}(t)dt\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\lim_{n\to\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\chi^{A^{\prime}}_{k}(i)\chi^{A^{\prime}}_{l}(i)\delta_{l,k}\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}(\chi^{A^{\prime}}_{k}(i))^{2}\right]=\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[(\chi^{A^{\prime}}_{k}(i))^{2}\right],

which implies
\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}{\mathbb{E}}\left[(\chi_{k}^{A^{\prime}}(i))^{2}\right]<\infty
. Similarly
\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}{\mathbb{E}}\left[(Z^{A^{\prime}}_{k})^{2}\right]<\infty
. Also,
S_{n}^{X^{A^{\prime}}(i)}\xrightarrow{L^{2}(dt\times d{\mathbb{P}}_{A^{\prime}})}X^{A}(i)
,

\displaystyle\lim_{n\to\infty}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(S_{n}^{X^{A^{\prime}}(i)}-X^{A^{\prime}}_{t}(i))^{2}dt\right]

\displaystyle=\lim_{n\to\infty}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\lim_{N\to\infty}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{n}\chi^{A^{\prime}}_{k}(i)\psi_{i,k}(t)-\sum_{k=1}^{N}\chi^{A^{\prime}}_{k}(i)\psi_{i,k}(t)\right)^{2}dt\right]

\displaystyle=\lim_{n\to\infty}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\lim_{N\to\infty}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(\sum_{k=n+1}^{N}\chi^{A^{\prime}}_{k}(i)\psi_{i,k}(t)\right)^{2}dt\right]

\displaystyle=\lim_{n\to\infty}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\lim_{N\to\infty}\sum_{k=n+1}^{N}(\chi^{A^{\prime}}_{k}(i))^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\lim_{n\to\infty}\sum_{k=n+1}^{\infty}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[(\chi^{A^{\prime}}_{k}(i))^{2}\right]=0,

(A.27)

by monotone convergence, and analogously
S_{n}^{Y^{A^{\prime}}}\xrightarrow{L^{2}(dt\times d{\mathbb{P}}_{A^{\prime}})}Y^{A^{\prime}}
. As
L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})
is separable and
\{\phi_{k}\}_{k\in{\mathbb{N}}}
and
\{\tilde{\psi}_{i,k}\}_{k\in{\mathbb{N}}}
are both ON-bases for
L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])
, it follows that
\{\phi_{k}\otimes\tilde{\psi}_{i,l}\}_{k,l}
is an ON-basis for
L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})
. Therefore

L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})=\left\{\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\lambda_{k,l}\phi_{k}\otimes\tilde{\psi}_{i,l}:\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\lambda_{k,l}^{2}<\infty\right\}.

Let

S_{n}^{\beta(i)}(t,\tau)=\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\lambda_{k,l}^{\beta(i),1}\psi_{i,l}(\tau)\phi_{k}(t)+\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\lambda_{k,l}^{\beta(i),2}\eta_{i,l}(\tau)\phi_{k}(t),

then
S_{n}^{\beta(i)}\xrightarrow{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}\beta(i)
.
By the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality,

\displaystyle\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)X^{A^{\prime}}_{\tau}(i)d\tau\right)^{2}dt

\displaystyle\leq\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left|(\beta(t,\tau))(i)X^{A^{\prime}}_{\tau}(i)\right|d\tau\right)^{2}dt

\displaystyle\leq\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left|(\beta(t,\tau))(i)\right|^{2}d\tau\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left|X^{A^{\prime}}_{\tau}(i)\right|^{2}d\tau\right)dt

\displaystyle=\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left|X^{A^{\prime}}_{\tau}(i)\right|^{2}d\tau\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left|(\beta(t,\tau))(i)\right|^{2}d\tau dt,

(A.28)

which is a.s. finite. Therefore if we let
Q_{i}(t)=\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)X^{A^{\prime}}_{\tau}(i)d\tau
and

S_{N}^{\int_{i}}(t)=\sum_{n=1}^{N}\left\langle\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(\beta(t,.))(i)X^{A^{\prime}}_{.}(i)d\tau,\phi_{k}\right\rangle_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}\phi_{k}(t)

then
S_{N}^{\int_{i}}\xrightarrow{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}Q_{i}
a.s.. Since

\displaystyle\left\langle\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(\beta(.,\tau))(i)X^{A^{\prime}}_{\tau}(i)d\tau,\phi_{k}\right\rangle_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}

\displaystyle=\lim_{n\to\infty}\sum_{k^{\prime}=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}\lambda_{k,l}^{\beta(i),1}\chi_{m}^{A^{\prime}}(i)\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\psi_{i,l}(\tau)\psi_{i,m}(\tau)\phi_{k^{\prime}}(t)\phi_{k}(t)d\tau dt

\displaystyle+\lim_{n\to\infty}\sum_{k^{\prime}=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}\lambda_{k,l}^{\beta(i),2}\chi_{m}^{A^{\prime}}(i)\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\eta_{i,l}(\tau)\psi_{i,m}(\tau)\phi_{k^{\prime}}(t)\phi_{k}(t)d\tau dt

\displaystyle=\lim_{n\to\infty}\sum_{k^{\prime}=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}\lambda_{k,l}^{\beta(i),1}\chi_{m}^{A^{\prime}}(i)\delta_{l,m}\delta_{k^{\prime},k}

\displaystyle=\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\lambda_{k,l}^{\beta(i),1}\chi_{l}^{A^{\prime}}(i)

we get,

\displaystyle S_{n}^{\int_{i}}(t)

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\lambda_{k,l}^{\beta(i)}\chi_{l}^{A^{\prime}}(i)\phi_{k}(t),

where we denote
\lambda_{k,l}^{\beta(i)}=\lambda_{k,l}^{\beta(i),1}
.
Next, utilizing (
A.5
),

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\lambda^{\beta(i)}_{k,l}\chi^{A^{\prime}}_{l}(i)\phi_{k}(t)-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(\beta(i))(t,\tau)X^{A^{\prime}}_{\tau}(i)d\tau\right)^{2}dt\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2^{p}\sum_{i=1}^{p}

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(S_{n}^{\int_{i}}(t)-\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{l=n+1}^{\infty}\lambda_{k,l}^{\beta(i)}\chi_{l}^{A^{\prime}}(i)\phi_{k}(t)-Q_{i}(t)\right)^{2}dt\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2^{p+1}\sum_{i=1}^{p}

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(S_{n}^{\int_{i}}(t)-Q_{i}(t)\right)^{2}dt\right]+2^{p+1}\sum_{i=1}^{p}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{l=n+1}^{\infty}\lambda_{k,l}^{\beta(i)}\chi_{l}^{A^{\prime}}(i)\phi_{k}(t)\right)^{2}dt\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2^{p+1}\sum_{i=1}^{p}

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(S_{n}^{\int_{i}}(t)-Q_{i}(t)\right)^{2}dt\right]+2^{p+1}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{l=n+1}^{\infty}\left(\lambda_{k,l}^{\beta(i)}\right)^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\left(\chi_{l}^{A^{\prime}}(i)\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2^{p+1}\sum_{i=1}^{p}

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(S_{n}^{\int_{i}}(t)-Q_{i}(t)\right)^{2}dt\right]+2^{p+1}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sup_{m\geq n+1}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\left(\chi_{m}^{A^{\prime}}(i)\right)^{2}\right]\lVert\beta(i)\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}

(A.29)

where the second on the right-most side tends to zero since
\sum_{m=1}^{\infty}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\left(\chi_{m}^{A^{\prime}}(i)\right)^{2}\right]<\infty
. Fixing
1\leq i\leq p
, we now bound the integral terms inside the expectation on the right-most side above using (
A.5
),

\displaystyle M_{n}:=\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(S_{n}^{\int_{i}}(t)-Q_{i}(t)\right)^{2}dt

\displaystyle\leq 2\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}Q_{i}(t)^{2}dt+2\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(S_{n}^{\int_{i}}(t)\right)^{2}dt

\displaystyle\leq 2\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left|X^{A^{\prime}}_{\tau}(i)\right|^{2}d\tau\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left|(\beta(t,\tau))(i)\right|^{2}d\tau dt

\displaystyle+2\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\lambda_{k,l}^{\beta(i)}\chi_{l}^{A^{\prime}}(i)\phi_{k}(t)\right)^{2}dt

\displaystyle=2\lVert X^{A^{\prime}}(i)\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}^{2}\lVert\beta\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p}}^{2}+2\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\lambda_{k,l}^{\beta(i)}\chi_{l}^{A^{\prime}}(i)\right)^{2}

(A.30)

and therefore

M_{n}\leq 2\lVert X^{A^{\prime}}(i)\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}^{2}\lVert\beta\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p}}^{2}+2\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\left(\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\lambda_{k,l}^{\beta(i)}\chi_{l}^{A^{\prime}}(i)\right)^{2}:=M.

Utilizing (
A.5
), we get

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[M\right]

\displaystyle=2\lVert X^{A^{\prime}}\rVert_{\mathcal{V}}^{2}\lVert\beta\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p}}^{2}+2\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\left(\lambda_{k,l}^{\beta(i)}\chi_{l}^{A^{\prime}}(i)\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=2\lVert X^{A^{\prime}}\rVert_{\mathcal{V}}^{2}\lVert\beta\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p}}^{2}+2\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\left(\lambda_{k,l}^{\beta(i)}\right)^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\left(\chi_{l}^{A^{\prime}}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 4\lVert X^{A^{\prime}}\rVert_{\mathcal{V}}^{2}\lVert\beta\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p}}^{2}<\infty

where we utilized

\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\left(\lambda_{k,l}^{\beta(i)}\right)^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\left(\chi_{l}^{A^{\prime}}(i)\right)^{2}\right]\leq\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\left(\chi_{l}^{A^{\prime}}(i)\right)^{2}\right]\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\left(\lambda_{k,l}^{\beta(i)}\right)^{2}\leq\lVert X^{A^{\prime}}\rVert_{\mathcal{V}}^{2}\lVert\beta\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p}}^{2}.

Since
\{M_{n}\}_{n\in{\mathbb{N}}}
converges to zero
{\mathbb{P}}_{A^{\prime}}
-a.s. and
0\leq M_{n}\leq M
it follows from the dominated convergence theorem that

\lim_{n\to\infty}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(S_{n}^{\int_{i}}(t)-Q_{i}(t)\right)^{2}dt\right]=0

and therefore due to (
A.5
) we get

\displaystyle\lim_{n\to\infty}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\lambda^{\beta(i)}_{k,l}\chi^{A^{\prime}}_{l}(i)\phi_{k}(t)-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(\beta(i))(t,\tau)X^{A^{\prime}}_{\tau}(i)d\tau\right)^{2}dt\right]=0.

(A.31)

Now we expand the risk function, for
A^{\prime}\in\mathcal{V}
, utilizing the fact that
S_{n}^{Y^{A^{\prime}}}\xrightarrow{L^{2}(dt\times d{\mathbb{P}}_{A^{\prime}})}Y^{A^{\prime}}
,

\displaystyle R_{A^{\prime}}(\beta)={\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(Y^{A^{\prime}}_{t}-\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\sum_{i=1}^{p}(\beta(i))(t,\tau)X^{A^{\prime}}(i)_{\tau}d\tau\right)^{2}dt\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\lim_{n\to\infty}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(S_{n}^{Y^{A^{\prime}}}(t)-Y^{A^{\prime}}_{t}\right)^{2}dt\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}+\lim_{n\to\infty}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(S_{n}^{Y^{A^{\prime}}}(t)-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\lambda^{\beta(i)}_{k,l}\chi^{A^{\prime}}_{l}(i)\phi_{k}(t)\right)^{2}dt\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+\lim_{n\to\infty}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\lambda^{\beta(i)}_{k,l}\chi^{A^{\prime}}_{l}(i)\phi_{k}(t)-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(\beta(i))(t,\tau)X^{A^{\prime}}_{\tau}(i)d\tau\right)^{2}dt\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}

and since the first and third term (due to (
A.31
)) converges to zero on the right-hand side above, we get

\displaystyle R_{A^{\prime}}(\beta)

\displaystyle=\lim_{n\to\infty}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{n}Z_{k}^{A^{\prime}}\phi_{k}(t)-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\lambda^{\beta(i)}_{k,l}\chi^{A^{\prime}}_{l}(i)\phi_{k}(t)\right)^{2}dt\right]

\displaystyle=\lim_{n\to\infty}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(Z_{k}^{A^{\prime}}-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\lambda_{k,l}^{\beta(i)}\chi_{l}^{A^{\prime}}(i)\right)^{2}\right].

(A.32)

From this point onwards we will fix our choice of ON-basis for
L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p+1}
, let

\phi_{1,m}=\phi_{m}
and

\phi_{i,m}=\tilde{\psi}_{i-1,m}
, for
2\leq i\leq p+1
and
m\in{\mathbb{N}}
.

Step 2: Reformulate the the integrals appearing in (
3.9
) for relevant subspaces

Denote
F_{i,k}(W)=\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}W_{t}\phi_{i,k}(t)dt
, for
W\in L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])
,
1\leq i\leq p+1
and for
A^{\prime}\in\mathcal{V}

\displaystyle F_{1:n}(A^{\prime})=

\displaystyle\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}A^{\prime}_{t}(1)\phi_{1,1}(t)dt,\ldots,\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}A^{\prime}(1)\phi_{1,n}(t)dt,\ldots,\right.

\displaystyle\left.\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}A^{\prime}_{t}(p+1)\phi_{p+1,1}(t)dt,\ldots,\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}A^{\prime}_{t}(p+1)\phi_{p+1,n}(t)dt\right).

Since
K_{A^{\prime}(i),A^{\prime}(j)},\in L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})
, for
1\leq i,j\leq p+1
(with the notation
K_{A^{\prime}(i),A^{\prime}(i)}=K_{A^{\prime}(i)}
), if we denote

K_{A^{\prime}}^{n}(s,t)=\begin{bmatrix}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}F_{1,k}(A^{\prime}(1))F_{1,l}(A^{\prime}(1))\phi_{1,k}(s)\phi_{1,l}(t)&\ldots&\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}F_{1,k}(A^{\prime}(1))F_{p+1,l}(A^{\prime}(p+1))\phi_{1,k}(s)\phi_{p+1,l}(t)\\
\vdots&\ddots&\vdots\\
\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}F_{1,k}(A^{\prime}(1))F_{p+1,l}(A^{\prime}(p+1))\phi_{1,k}(s)\phi_{p+1,l}(t)&\ldots&\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}F_{p+1,k}(A^{\prime}(p+1))F_{p+1,l}(A^{\prime}(p+1))\phi_{p+1,k}(s)\phi_{p+1,l}(t)\end{bmatrix},

(A.33)

then all the elements of the matrix
K_{A^{\prime}}^{n}
converges in
L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})
to the corresponding elements of
K_{A^{\prime}}
. With a bit of abuse of notation let
K_{i,j}
and
K^{n}_{i,j}
denote the element on row
i
and column
j
of the matrix
K_{A^{\prime}}
and
K_{A^{\prime}}^{n}
respectively. Fix
n\in{\mathbb{N}}
and let
\textbf{g}(s)=\left(g_{1}(s),....,g_{p+1}(s)\right)
where
g_{i}\in span\left\{\phi_{i,1},\ldots,\phi_{i,n}\right\}
, for
1\leq i\leq p+1
. Then

\displaystyle\lim_{m\to\infty}\left|\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]^{2}}\textbf{g}(s)\left(K_{A^{\prime}}(s,t)-K^{m}_{A^{\prime}}(s,t)\right)\textbf{g}(t)^{T}dsdt\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lim_{m\to\infty}\left|\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]^{2}}\langle\textbf{g}(s),\textbf{g}(t)\left(K_{A^{\prime}}(s,t)-K^{m}_{A^{\prime}}(s,t)\right)\rangle_{{\mathbb{R}}^{p+1}}dsdt\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\lim_{m\to\infty}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]^{2}}\left|\langle\textbf{g}(s),\textbf{g}(t)\left(K_{A^{\prime}}(s,t)-K^{m}_{A^{\prime}}(s,t)\right)\rangle_{{\mathbb{R}}^{p+1}}\right|dsdt

\displaystyle\leq\lim_{m\to\infty}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]^{2}}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{p+1}g_{i}(s)^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{p+1}g_{i}(t)^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left\lVert K_{A^{\prime}}(s,t)-K^{m}_{A^{\prime}}(s,t)\right\rVert_{\mathit{l}^{2}}dsdt

\displaystyle\leq\lim_{m\to\infty}T\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{p+1}g_{i}(s)^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{p+1}g_{i}(t)^{2}dsdt\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]^{2}}\left\lVert K_{A^{\prime}}(s,t)-K^{m}_{A^{\prime}}(s,t)\right\rVert_{\mathit{l}^{2}}^{2}dsdt\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\lim_{m\to\infty}dT\sum_{i=1}^{p+1}\int g_{i}(t)^{2}dt\left(\sum_{i=1}^{p+1}\sum_{j=1}^{p+1}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]^{2}}\left|K_{i,j}(s,t)-K^{m}_{i,j}(s,t)\right|^{2}dsdt\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle=\lim_{m\to\infty}dT\sum_{i=1}^{p+1}\lVert g_{i}\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}^{2}\left(\lim_{m\to\infty}\sum_{i=1}^{p+1}\sum_{j=1}^{p+1}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]^{2}}\left|K_{i,j}(s,t)-K^{m}_{i,j}(s,t)\right|^{2}dsdt\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}=0,

(A.34)

for some constant
d
that only depends on
p
and where we utilized the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality (both on
{\mathbb{R}}^{p+1}
as well for the product integral) and that

\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]^{2}}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{p+1}g_{i}(s)^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{p+1}g_{i}(t)^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}dsdt\leq T\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{p+1}g_{i}(s)^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{p+1}g_{i}(t)^{2}dsdt\right)^{\frac{1}{2}},

which follows from Jensen’s inequality applied to the measure
\frac{1}{T^{2}}dsdt
on
[T_{1},T_{2}]^{2}
. Utilizing (
A.5
) and the orthogonality,

\displaystyle\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]^{2}}\textbf{g}(s)\left(K_{A^{\prime}}(s,t)-K_{A^{\prime}}^{n}(s,t)\right)\textbf{g}(t)^{T}dsdt

\displaystyle=\lim_{m\to\infty}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]^{2}}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{n}F_{1,k}(g_{1})\phi_{1,k}(s),...,\sum_{k=1}^{n}F_{p+1,k}(g_{p+1})\phi_{p+1,k}(s)\right)\left((K_{A^{\prime}}^{m}(s,t)-K_{A^{\prime}}^{n}(s,t))\right)

\displaystyle\times\left(\sum_{k=1}^{n}F_{1,k}(g_{1})\phi_{1,k}(t),...,\sum_{k=1}^{n}F_{p+1,k}(g_{p+1})\phi_{p+1,k}(t)\right)^{T}dsdt

\displaystyle=\lim_{m\to\infty}\sum_{i=1}^{p+1}\sum_{j=1}^{p+1}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}F_{i,k}(g_{i})F_{j,l}(g_{j})\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]^{2}}\left(\phi_{j,l}(s)\phi_{i,k}(t)\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left(\sum_{k^{\prime}=1}^{m}\sum_{l^{\prime}=1}^{m}F_{i,k^{\prime}}(A^{\prime}(i))F_{j,l^{\prime}}(A^{\prime}(j))\phi_{j,l^{\prime}}(s)\phi_{i,k^{\prime}}(t)-\sum_{k^{\prime}=1}^{n}\sum_{l^{\prime}=1}^{n}F_{i,k^{\prime}}(A^{\prime}(i))F_{j,l^{\prime}}(A^{\prime}(j))\phi_{j,l^{\prime}}(s)\phi_{i,k^{\prime}}(t)\right)\right)dsdt=0.

Therefore

\displaystyle\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]^{2}}\left(g_{1}(s),....,g_{p+1}(s)\right)K_{A^{\prime}}(s,t)\left(g_{1}(t),....,g_{p+1}(t)\right)^{T}dsdt

\displaystyle=\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]^{2}}\left(g_{1}(s),....,g_{p+1}(s)\right)K_{A^{\prime}}^{n}(s,t)\left(g_{1}(t),....,g_{p+1}(t)\right)^{T}dsdt.

Step 3: Compute a finite dimensional approximation of the target and the covariates and the corresponding error to this approximation

Let
V_{n}=\mathsf{span}\left(\left\{(\phi_{1,i_{1}},0,\ldots,0),(0,\phi_{2,i_{2}},0,\ldots,0),\ldots,(0,\ldots,0,\phi_{p+1,i_{k}})\right\}_{1\leq i_{1},...,i_{k}\leq n}\right)
and
P_{n}
denote projection on the space
V_{n}
. For any
a\in L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p+1}
we have

\displaystyle\lVert P_{n}\mathcal{S}a-P_{n}\mathcal{S}P_{n}a\rVert

\displaystyle=\lVert P_{n}\mathcal{S}\rVert\lVert a-P_{n}a\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\lVert\mathcal{S}\rVert\lVert a-P_{n}a\rVert,

(A.35)

which converges to zero, since
\mathcal{H}^{p+1}\left(\{\phi_{i,n}\}_{n\in{\mathbb{N}},1\leq i\leq p+1}\right)
is a basis for
L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p+1}
and due to the definition of
V_{n}
(this just comes down to convergence of the partial sums). Enumerate
\mathcal{H}^{p+1}\left(\{\phi_{i,n}\}_{n\in{\mathbb{N}},1\leq i\leq p+1}\right)
so that
e_{1}=\left(\phi_{1,1},0,\ldots,0\right)
and
e_{n(p+1)}=\left(0,\ldots,0,\phi_{p+1,n}\right)
. Let
x=\sum_{k=1}^{n(p+1)}a_{k}e_{k}\in V_{n}
, then

\displaystyle P_{n}\mathcal{S}x=P_{n}\mathcal{S}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{n(p+1)}a_{k}e_{k}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{n(p+1)}a_{k}P_{n}\mathcal{S}\left(e_{k}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{m=1}^{n(p+1)}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{n(p+1)}a_{k}\langle\mathcal{S}\left(e_{k}\right),e_{m}\rangle\right)e_{m},

so the coordinates of
P_{n}\mathcal{S}x
in the basis
\mathcal{H}^{p+1}\left(\{\phi_{i,n}\}_{n\in{\mathbb{N}},1\leq i\leq p+1}\right)
are given by

\left(\left(\sum_{k=1}^{n(p+1)}a_{k}\langle\mathcal{S}\left(e_{k}\right),e_{1}\rangle\right),\ldots,\left(\sum_{k=1}^{n(p+1)}a_{k}\langle\mathcal{S}\left(e_{k}\right),e_{n(p+1)}\rangle\right)\right).

So by defining the following
n(p+1)\times n(p+1)
matrix

B^{n}=\begin{bmatrix}\langle\mathcal{S}\left(\phi_{1,1},0,\ldots,0\right),\left(\phi_{1,1},0,\ldots,0\right)\rangle&\ldots&\langle\mathcal{S}\left(0,\ldots,0,\phi_{p+1,n}\right),\left(\phi_{1,1},0,\ldots,0\right)\rangle\\
\vdots&\ddots&\vdots\\
\langle\mathcal{S}\left(\phi_{1,1},0,\ldots,0\right),\left(0,\ldots,0,\phi_{p+1,n}\right)\rangle&\ldots&\langle\mathcal{S}\left(0,\ldots,0,\phi_{p+1,n}\right),\left(0,\ldots,0,\phi_{p+1,n}\right)\rangle\end{bmatrix},

(A.36)

we have that if
x\in V_{n}
and we let
H(x)
denote coordinates of
x
in the basis
\mathcal{H}^{p+1}\left(\{\phi_{i,n}\}_{n\in{\mathbb{N}},1\leq i\leq p+1}\right)
, then
H\left(P_{n}\mathcal{S}x\right)=B^{n}H(x)
. For
A^{\prime}\in\mathcal{V}
, let
\mathbf{\chi}_{n}=\left(\chi_{1}^{A^{\prime}}(1),\ldots,\chi_{n}^{A^{\prime}}(1),\ldots,\chi_{n}^{A^{\prime}}(p)\right)
and
\mathbf{Z}^{n}=\left(Z_{1}^{A^{\prime}},\ldots,Z_{n}^{A^{\prime}}\right)
. We have
(\mathbf{Z}^{n},\mathbf{\chi}^{n})=H\left(P_{n}(Y^{A^{\prime}},X^{A^{\prime}})\right)
and
B^{n}\left(F_{1:n}(A^{\prime})+F_{1:n}(\epsilon^{A^{\prime}})\right)=H(P_{n}\mathcal{S}P_{n}(A^{\prime}+\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}))
(since
F_{1:n}(A^{\prime})+F_{1:n}(\epsilon^{A^{\prime}})=P_{n}(A^{\prime}+\epsilon^{A^{\prime}})
). Therefore,

\displaystyle\lVert(\mathbf{Z}^{n},\mathbf{\chi}^{n})-B^{n}(F_{1:n}(A^{\prime})+F_{1:n}(\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}))\rVert_{\left(\mathit{l^{2}}\right)^{p+1}}

\displaystyle=\lVert P_{n}(Y^{A^{\prime}},X^{A^{\prime}})-P_{n}\mathcal{S}P_{n}(A^{\prime}+\epsilon^{A^{\prime}})\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p+1}}

\displaystyle=\lVert P_{n}\mathcal{S}(A^{\prime}+\epsilon^{A^{\prime}})-P_{n}\mathcal{S}P_{n}(A^{\prime}+\epsilon^{A^{\prime}})\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p+1}},

which converges path-wise (per
\omega
) to zero as
n\to\infty
, due to (
A.5
).
This implies

\begin{bmatrix}Z_{1}^{A^{\prime}}\\
\vdots\\
Z_{n}^{A^{\prime}}\\
\chi_{1}^{A^{\prime}}(1)\\
\vdots\\
\chi_{n}^{A^{\prime}}(1)\\
\vdots\\
\chi_{n}^{A^{\prime}}(p)\ \end{bmatrix}=B^{n}\cdot\left(\begin{bmatrix}F_{1,}(A^{\prime}(1))\\
\vdots\\
F_{1,n}(A^{\prime}(1))\\
F_{2,1}(A^{\prime}(2))\\
\vdots\\
F_{2,n}(A^{\prime}(2))\\
\vdots\\
F_{p+1,n}(A^{\prime}(p+1))\end{bmatrix}+\begin{bmatrix}F_{1,1}(\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}(1))\\
\vdots\\
F_{1,n}(\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}(1))\\
F_{2,1}(\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}(2))\\
\vdots\\
F_{2,n}(\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}(2))\\
\vdots\\
F_{p+1,n}(\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}(p+1))\end{bmatrix}\right)+\delta_{n}(A^{\prime}),

(A.37)

where

\displaystyle\lVert\delta_{n}(A^{\prime})\rVert_{\left(\mathit{l^{2}}\right)^{p+1}}

\displaystyle=\lVert P_{n}\mathcal{S}(A^{\prime}+\epsilon^{A^{\prime}})-P_{n}\mathcal{S}P_{n}(A^{\prime}+\epsilon^{A^{\prime}})\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p+1}}

\displaystyle\leq\lVert\mathcal{S}\rVert\left(\lVert A^{\prime}-P_{n}A^{\prime}\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p+1}}+\lVert\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}-P_{n}\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p+1}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2\lVert\mathcal{S}\rVert\left(\lVert A^{\prime}\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p+1}}+\lVert\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p+1}}\right).

(A.38)

Therefore

\displaystyle\lVert\delta_{n}(A^{\prime})\rVert_{\left(\mathit{l^{2}}\right)^{p+1}}^{2}\leq 4\lVert\mathcal{S}\rVert^{2}\left(\lVert A^{\prime}\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p+1}}^{2}+\lVert\epsilon^{A^{\prime}}\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p+1}}^{2}\right).

By dominated convergence, this implies
{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\lVert\delta_{n}(A^{\prime})\rVert_{\left(\mathit{l^{2}}\right)^{p+1}}^{2}\right]\to 0
.

Step 4: Approximate the risk using the finite dimensional approximation from the previous step

Let
\textbf{v}_{n}=\sum_{k=1}^{n}B^{n}_{k,.}-\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\lambda_{k,l}^{\beta(i)}B^{n}_{in+l,.}
. From (
A.5
) we have that for any
A^{\prime\prime}\in\mathcal{V}
,

\displaystyle R_{A^{\prime\prime}}(\beta)

\displaystyle=\lim_{n\to\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\left(Z_{k}^{A^{\prime\prime}}-\sum_{l=1}^{n}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\lambda_{k,l}^{\beta(i)}\chi_{l}^{A^{\prime\prime}}(i)\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\lim_{n\to\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\left(B^{n}_{k,.}(F_{1:n}(A^{\prime\prime})^{T}+F_{1:n}(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}})^{T})+(\delta_{n}(A^{\prime\prime}))(k)\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\lambda_{k,l}^{\beta(i)}\left(B_{in+l,.}(F_{1:n}(A^{\prime\prime})^{T}+F_{1:n}(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}})^{T})+(\delta_{n}(A^{\prime\prime}))(in+l)\right)\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\lim_{n\to\infty}\left(\textbf{v}_{n}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\left(F_{1:n}(A^{\prime\prime})+F_{1:n}(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}})\right)^{T}\left(F_{1:n}(A^{\prime\prime})+F_{1:n}(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}})\right)\right]\textbf{v}_{n}^{T}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\sum_{k=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\left((\delta_{n}(A^{\prime\prime}))(k)+\sum_{l=1}^{n}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\lambda_{k,l}^{\beta(i)}(\delta_{n}(A^{\prime\prime}))(in+l)\right)^{2}\right]\right.

\displaystyle\left.+2\sum_{k=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\left(B^{n}_{k,.}(F_{1:n}(A^{\prime\prime})^{T}+F_{1:n}(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}})^{T})-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\lambda_{k,l}^{\beta(i)}\left(B_{in+l,.}(F_{1:n}(A^{\prime\prime})^{T}+F_{1:n}(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}})^{T})\right)\right)\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.\cdot\left((\delta_{n}(A^{\prime\prime}))(k)+\sum_{l=1}^{n}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\lambda_{k,l}^{\beta(i)}(\delta_{n}(A^{\prime\prime}))(in+l)\right)\right]\right).

(A.39)

The term

\displaystyle 2\sum_{k=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}

\displaystyle\left[\left(B^{n}_{k,.}(F_{1:n}(A^{\prime\prime})^{T}+F_{1:n}(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}})^{T})-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\lambda_{k,l}^{\beta(i)}\left(B_{in+l,.}(F(A^{\prime\prime})^{T}+F(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}})^{T})\right)\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.\cdot\left((\delta_{n}(A^{\prime\prime}))(k)+\sum_{l=1}^{n}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\lambda_{k,l}^{\beta(i)}(\delta_{n}(A^{\prime\prime}))(in+l)\right)\right]

is readily dominated by (using the Cauchy Schwarz-inequality, first for the expectation and then for the sum)

\displaystyle 2\sum_{k=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\left(B^{n}_{k,.}(F_{1:n}(A^{\prime\prime})^{T}+F_{1:n}(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}})^{T})-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\lambda_{k,l}^{\beta(i)}\left(B_{in+l,.}(F_{1:n}(A^{\prime\prime})^{T}+F_{1:n}(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}})^{T})\right)\right)^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\cdot{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\left((\delta_{n}(A^{\prime\prime}))(k)+\sum_{l=1}^{n}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\lambda_{k,l}^{\beta(i)}(\delta_{n}(A^{\prime\prime}))(in+l)\right)^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left(\sum_{k=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\left(B^{n}_{k,.}(F(A^{\prime\prime})^{T}+F(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}})^{T})-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\lambda_{k,l}^{\beta(i)}\left(B_{in+l,.}(F_{1:n}(A^{\prime\prime})^{T}+F_{1:n}(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}})^{T})\right)\right)^{2}\right]\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\times\left(\sum_{k=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\left((\delta_{n}(A^{\prime\prime}))(k)+\sum_{l=1}^{n}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\lambda_{k,l}^{\beta(i)}(\delta_{n}(A^{\prime\prime}))(in+l)\right)^{2}\right]\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

This term will converge to zero since, as we will see,

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\left((\delta_{n}(A^{\prime\prime}))(k)+\sum_{l=1}^{n}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\lambda_{k,l}^{\beta(i)}(\delta_{n}(A^{\prime\prime}))(in+l)\right)^{2}\right]

(A.40)

converges to zero, while we will show that the term

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\left(B^{n}_{k,.}(F_{1:n}(A^{\prime\prime})^{T}+F_{1:n}(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}})^{T})-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\lambda_{k,l}^{\beta(i)}\left(B_{in+l,.}(F_{1:n}(A^{\prime\prime})^{T}+F_{1:n}(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}})^{T})\right)\right)^{2}\right],

(A.41)

is bounded. First, we will show that (
A.40
) converges to zero. Expanding the squares we find

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\left((\delta_{n}(A^{\prime\prime}))(k)+\sum_{l=1}^{n}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\lambda_{k,l}^{\beta(i)}(\delta_{n}(A^{\prime\prime}))(in+l)\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\lVert(\delta_{n}(A^{\prime\prime}))\rVert_{\mathit{l}^{2}}^{2}\right]+2\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\lambda_{k,l}^{\beta(i)}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\delta_{n}(k)(\delta_{n}(A^{\prime\prime}))(in+l)\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{p}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{p}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{l_{1}=1}^{n}\sum_{l_{2}=1}^{n}\lambda_{k,l_{1}}^{\beta(i_{1})}\lambda_{k,l_{2}}^{\beta(i_{1})}\lambda_{k,l_{1}}^{\beta(i_{2})}\lambda_{k,l_{2}}^{\beta(i_{2})}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[(\delta_{n}(A^{\prime\prime}))(in+l_{1})(\delta_{n}(A^{\prime\prime}))(in+l_{2})\right],

(A.42)

where we already know that the first term on the right-most side will vanish. We then bound the second term in (
A.5
),

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\lambda_{k,l}^{\beta(i)}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[(\delta_{n}(A^{\prime\prime}))(k)(\delta_{n}(A^{\prime\prime}))(in+l)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(\lambda_{k,l}^{\beta(i)}\right)^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\sum_{i=1}^{p}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[(\delta_{n}(A^{\prime\prime}))(k)(\delta_{n}(A^{\prime\prime}))(in+l)\right]^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{i=1}^{p}\lVert\beta(i)\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\sum_{i=1}^{p}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[(\delta_{n}(A^{\prime\prime}))(k)^{2}\right]{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[(\delta_{n}(A^{\prime\prime}))(in+l)^{2}\right]\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{k=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[(\delta_{n}(A^{\prime\prime}))(k)^{2}\right]\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\lVert\beta\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})^{p+1}}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{l=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[(\delta_{n}(A^{\prime\prime}))(in+l)^{2}\right]\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\lVert(\delta_{n}(A^{\prime\prime}))\rVert_{\left(\mathit{l^{2}}\right)^{p+1}}^{2}\right]\lVert\beta\rVert_{\left(L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})\right)^{p}},

which converges to zero. For the third term of (
A.5
), for fixed
1\leq i_{1},i_{2}\leq p

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{l_{1}=1}^{n}\sum_{l_{2}=1}^{n}\lambda_{k,l_{1}}^{\beta(i_{1})}\lambda_{k,l_{2}}^{\beta(i_{1})}\lambda_{k,l_{1}}^{\beta(i_{2})}\lambda_{k,l_{2}}^{\beta(i_{2})}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[(\delta_{n}(A^{\prime\prime}))(in+l_{1})(\delta_{n}(A^{\prime\prime}))(in+l_{2})\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{l_{1}=1}^{n}\sum_{l_{2}=1}^{n}\left|\lambda_{k,l_{1}}^{\beta(i_{1})}\right|\left|\lambda_{k,l_{2}}^{\beta(i_{1})}\right|\left|\lambda_{k,l_{1}}^{\beta(i_{2})}\right|\left|\lambda_{k,l_{2}}^{\beta(i_{2})}\right|{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\left|(\delta_{n}(A^{\prime\prime}))(in+l_{1})(\delta_{n}(A^{\prime\prime}))(in+l_{2})\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{l_{1}=1}^{n}\sum_{l_{2}=1}^{n}\left|\lambda_{k,l_{1}}^{\beta(i_{1})}\right|\left|\lambda_{k,l_{2}}^{\beta(i_{1})}\right|\left|\lambda_{k,l_{1}}^{\beta(i_{2})}\right|\left|\lambda_{k,l_{2}}^{\beta(i_{2})}\right|{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[(\delta_{n}(A^{\prime\prime}))(in+l_{1})^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[(\delta_{n}(A^{\prime\prime}))(in+l_{2})^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(\sum_{l=1}^{n}\left|\lambda_{k,l}^{\beta(i_{1})}\right|\left|\lambda_{k,l}^{\beta(i_{2})}\right|{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[(\delta_{n}(A^{\prime\prime}))(in+l)^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\left|\lambda_{k,l}^{\beta(i_{1})}\right|^{2}\left|\lambda_{k,l}^{\beta(i_{2})}\right|^{2}\sum_{l=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[(\delta_{n}(A^{\prime\prime}))(in+l)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\lVert\delta_{n}(A^{\prime\prime})\rVert_{\left(\mathit{l^{2}}\right)^{p+1}}^{2}\right]\left(\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\left|\lambda_{k,l}^{\beta(i_{1})}\right|^{4}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\left|\lambda_{k,l}^{\beta(i_{2})}\right|^{4}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\lVert\delta_{n}(A^{\prime\prime})\rVert_{\left(\mathit{l^{2}}\right)^{p+1}}^{2}\right]\left(\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\left(\lambda_{k,l}^{\beta(i_{1})}\right)^{2}\right)\left(\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\left(\lambda_{k,l}^{\beta(i_{1})}\right)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\lVert\delta_{n}(A^{\prime\prime})\rVert_{\left(\mathit{l^{2}}\right)^{p+1}}^{2}\right]\lVert\beta(i_{1})\rVert_{\mathit{L}^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}^{2}\lVert\beta(i_{2})\rVert_{\mathit{L}^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}^{2},

which also converges to zero and where we used the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality for expectations as well as sums and the fact that the
\mathit{l}^{4}
-norm is dominated by the
\mathit{l}^{2}
-norm. Summing over
i_{1}
and
i_{2}
, this will still converge to zero. We shall now establish that (
A.41
) is indeed bounded. We have,

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\left(B^{n}_{k,.}(F_{1:n}(A^{\prime\prime})+F_{1:n}(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}))-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\lambda_{k,l}^{\beta(i)}\left(B_{in+l,.}(F_{1:n}(A^{\prime\prime})^{T}+F_{1:n}(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}})^{T})\right)\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[2\left(B^{n}_{k,.}(F_{1:n}(A^{\prime\prime})^{T}+F_{1:n}(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}})^{T})\right)^{2}\right]+2\sum_{k=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\left(\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\lambda_{k,l}^{\beta(i)}\left\langle B_{in+l,.},\left((F_{1:n}(A^{\prime\prime})^{T}+F_{1:n}(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}})^{T})\right)\right\rangle\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k=1}^{n}4{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\left(Z_{k}^{A^{\prime\prime}}\right)^{2}+(\delta_{n}(A^{\prime\prime}))(k)^{2}\right]+\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\sum_{i=1}^{p}2\left(\lambda_{k,l}^{\beta(i)}\right)^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\left\lVert B^{n}_{in+1:(i+1)n,.}(F_{1:n}(A^{\prime\prime})^{T}+F_{1:n}(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}})^{T})\right\rVert_{\mathit{l}^{2}}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k=1}^{n}4{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\left(Z_{k}^{A^{\prime\prime}}\right)^{2}\right]+4{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\lVert\delta_{n}(A^{\prime\prime})\rVert_{\left(\mathit{l^{2}}\right)^{p+1}}^{2}\right]+2\lVert\beta\rVert_{\left(L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})\right)^{p}}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{p}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\sum_{l=1}^{n}\left(\chi^{A^{\prime\prime}}_{l}(i)-(\delta_{n}(A^{\prime\prime}))(in+l)\right)^{2}\right],

where we utilized (
A.37
). As

{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\sum_{l=1}^{n}\left(\chi^{A^{\prime\prime}}_{l}(i)-(\delta_{n}(A^{\prime\prime}))(in+l)\right)^{2}\right]\leq 2\sum_{l=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\left(\chi^{A^{\prime\prime}}_{l}(i)\right)^{2}\right]+2{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\left\lVert\delta_{n}(A^{\prime\prime})\right\rVert_{\left(\mathit{l^{2}}\right)^{p+1}}^{2}\right]<\infty,

we then readily see that (
A.41
) is indeed bounded. Returning to (
A.5
) we now have
for any
A^{\prime\prime}\in\mathcal{V}
,

\displaystyle R_{A^{\prime\prime}}(\beta)

\displaystyle=\lim_{n\to\infty}\textbf{v}_{n}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\left(F_{1:n}(A^{\prime\prime})+F_{1:n}(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}})\right)\left(F_{1:n}(A^{\prime\prime})+F_{1:n}(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}})\right)^{T}\right]\textbf{v}_{n}^{T}.

(A.43)

Step 5: Verify that the cross terms between the shifts and the noise vanish:

Claim A.1
.

Let
G_{1},G_{2}\in(L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p+1})^{*}
then we have that

{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[G_{1}(A^{\prime})G_{2}(\epsilon^{A^{\prime}})\right]={\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[G_{1}(A^{\prime\prime})G_{2}(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}})\right]=0

Proof of Claim
A.1
.

We show
{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[G_{1}(A^{\prime\prime})G_{2}(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}})\right]=0
,
{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[G_{1}(A^{\prime})G_{2}(\epsilon^{A^{\prime}})\right]=0
is analogous. Let
\epsilon_{A^{\prime}}^{n}
,
\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}^{n}
,
A^{\prime n}
and
A^{\prime\prime n}
be defined as in the proof of Lemma
3.1
. Since

\displaystyle\left|G_{1}(A^{\prime\prime n})G_{2}(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}^{n})-G_{1}(A^{\prime\prime})G_{2}(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}})\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|G_{1}(A^{\prime\prime n})G_{2}(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}^{n})-G_{1}(A^{\prime\prime n})G_{2}(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}})\right|+\left|G_{1}(A^{\prime\prime n})G_{2}(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}})-G_{1}(A^{\prime\prime})G_{2}(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}})\right|

\displaystyle\leq\lVert G_{1}\rVert\lVert G_{2}\rVert\lVert A^{\prime\prime n}\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p+1}}\lVert\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}^{n}-\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p+1}}

\displaystyle+\lVert G_{1}\rVert\lVert G_{2}\rVert\lVert A^{\prime\prime n}-A^{\prime\prime}\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p+1}}\lVert\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p+1}}

it follows from the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality that

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\left|G_{1}(A^{\prime\prime n})G_{2}(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}^{n})-G_{1}(A^{\prime\prime})G_{2}(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}})\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq\lVert G_{1}\rVert\lVert G_{2}\rVert{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\lVert A^{\prime\prime n}\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p+1}}^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\lVert\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}^{n}-\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p+1}}^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+\lVert G_{1}\rVert\lVert G_{2}\rVert{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\lVert A^{\prime\prime n}-A^{\prime\prime}\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p+1}}^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\lVert\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p+1}}^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}},

which converges to zero. Next,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[G_{1}\left(A^{\prime\prime n}\right)G_{2}\left(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}^{n}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{N_{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{N_{n}}{\mathbb{P}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left(\left\{A^{\prime\prime}\in Q_{j}^{n}\right\}\cap\left\{\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}\in Q_{j}^{n}\right\}\right)G_{1}\left(w^{n}_{j}\right)G_{2}\left(w^{n}_{j}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{N_{n}}{\mathbb{P}}\left(A^{\prime\prime}\in Q_{j}^{n}\right)G_{1}\left(w^{n}_{j}\right)\sum_{i=1}^{N_{n}}{\mathbb{P}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}\in Q_{j}^{n}\right)G_{2}\left(w^{n}_{j}\right)

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[G_{1}\left(A^{\prime\prime n}\right)\right]{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[G_{2}\left(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}^{n}\right)\right]={\mathbb{E}}\left[G_{1}\left(A^{\prime\prime n}\right)\right]G_{2}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}^{n}\right]\right),

Therefore

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[G_{1}(A^{\prime\prime})G_{2}(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}})\right]

\displaystyle=\lim_{n\to\infty}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[G_{1}(A^{\prime\prime n})G_{2}(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}^{n})\right]

\displaystyle=\lim_{n\to\infty}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[G_{1}(A^{\prime\prime n})\right]{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[G_{2}(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}^{n})\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[G_{1}(A^{\prime\prime})\right]\lim_{n\to\infty}G_{2}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}^{n}\right]\right)

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[G_{1}(A^{\prime\prime})\right]G_{2}\left(\lim_{n\to\infty}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}^{n}\right]\right)

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[G_{1}(A^{\prime\prime})\right]G_{2}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}\right]\right)=0,

where the fact that
G_{2}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}^{n}\right]\right)={\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[G_{2}\left(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}^{n}\right)\right]
follows from the linearity of
G_{2}
.
∎

We now claim that
F_{k}\circ S_{i}\in(L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p+1})^{*}
for any
k\in{\mathbb{N}},1\leq i\leq p+1
. Taking
g=\left(g_{1},\ldots,g_{p+1}\right)\in L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p+1}
we have by the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality

\displaystyle\left|F_{i,k}\left(S_{i}g\right)\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}g_{i}(t)\phi_{i,k}(t)dt\right|

\displaystyle\leq\lVert g_{i}\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}\leq\lVert g\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p+1}}.

Therefore, by Claim
A.1
with
G_{1}=F_{i,k}\circ S_{i}
and
G_{2}=F_{j,l}\circ S_{j}
,
{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[F_{i,k}(A^{\prime\prime}(i))F_{j,l}(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}(j))\right]=0
for all
k,l\in{\mathbb{N}}
and
1\leq i,j,\leq p+1
. This implies

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[F_{1:n}(A^{\prime\prime})F_{1:n}(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}})^{T}\right]=0,\hskip 5.69054pt\forall n\in{\mathbb{N}}.

(A.44)

Step 6: Isolate the pure observational term

Claim A.2
.

Let
G_{1},G_{2}\in(L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p+1})^{*}
(i.e. a bounded linear functional on
L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p+1}
). Then

{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[G_{1}(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}})G_{2}(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}})\right]={\mathbb{E}}\left[G_{1}(\epsilon)G_{2}(\epsilon)\right]={\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[G_{1}(\epsilon^{A^{\prime}})G_{2}(\epsilon^{A^{\prime}})\right].

Proof of Claim
A.2
.

We will show the first equality, the second one is analogous. Let
\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}^{n}
be as above and let
\epsilon^{n}=\sum_{j=1}^{N_{n}}w_{j}^{n}1_{\epsilon\in Q_{j}^{n}}
, where
W_{e}^{n}=\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}w_{j}^{n}1_{\epsilon\in Q_{j}^{n}}
and we now choose
N_{n}
as in the proof of Lemma
3.1
but also large enough to assure that
