{\mathbb{E}}\left[\lVert\epsilon^{n}-W_{e}^{n}\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p+1}}^{2}\right]=\sum_{j=N_{n}+1}^{\infty}\|w_{j}^{n}\|^{2}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\epsilon\in Q_{j}^{n}\right)<\frac{1}{2n}.

This will also imply
{\mathbb{E}}\left[\lVert\epsilon^{n}-\epsilon\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p+1}}^{2}\right]\to 0
. We note that

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[G_{1}\left(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}^{n}\right)G_{2}\left(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}^{n}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{N_{n}}{\mathbb{P}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}\in Q_{i}^{n}\right)G_{1}\left(w^{n}_{i}\right)G_{2}\left(w^{n}_{i}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{N_{n}}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\epsilon\in Q_{i}^{n}\right)\prod_{j=1}^{2}G_{1}\left(w^{n}_{i}\right)G_{2}\left(w^{n}_{i}\right)={\mathbb{E}}\left[G_{1}\left(\epsilon^{n}\right)G_{2}\left(\epsilon^{n}\right)\right].

(A.45)

Since

\displaystyle\left|{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[G_{1}\left(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}^{n}\right)G_{2}\left(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}^{n}\right)\right]-{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[G_{1}\left(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}\right)G_{2}\left(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\left|G_{2}\left(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}^{n}\right)G_{1}\left(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}^{n}-\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}\right)\right|\right]+{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\left|G_{1}\left(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}\right)G_{2}\left(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}^{n}-\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}\right)\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq\lVert G_{2}\rVert\lVert G_{1}\rVert{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\lVert\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}^{n}\rVert_{\left(L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])\right)^{p+1}}\lVert\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}^{n}-\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}\rVert_{\left(L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])\right)^{p+1}}\right]

\displaystyle+\lVert G_{2}\rVert\lVert G_{1}\rVert{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\lVert\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}\rVert_{\left(L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])\right)^{p+1}}\lVert\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}^{n}-\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}\rVert_{\left(L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])\right)^{p+1}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\lVert G_{2}\rVert\lVert G_{1}\rVert\left({\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\lVert\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}\rVert_{\left(L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])\right)^{p+1}}^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}+{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\lVert\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}^{n}\rVert_{\left(L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])\right)^{p+1}}^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}\right){\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\lVert\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}^{n}-\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}\rVert_{\left(L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])\right)^{p+1}}^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}

(A.46)

and analogously

\displaystyle\left|{\mathbb{E}}\left[G_{1}\left(\epsilon^{n}\right)G_{2}\left(\epsilon^{n}\right)\right]-{\mathbb{E}}\left[G_{1}\left(\epsilon\right)G_{2}\left(\epsilon\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\lVert G_{2}\rVert\lVert G_{1}\rVert\left({\mathbb{E}}\left[\lVert\epsilon\rVert_{\left(L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])\right)^{p+1}}^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}+{\mathbb{E}}\left[\lVert\epsilon^{n}\rVert_{\left(L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])\right)^{p+1}}^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\lVert\epsilon^{n}-\epsilon\rVert_{\left(L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])\right)^{p+1}}^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}.

(A.47)

Next,

\displaystyle\left|{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[G_{1}\left(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}\right)G_{2}\left(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}\right)\right]-{\mathbb{E}}\left[G_{1}\left(\epsilon\right)G_{2}\left(\epsilon\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[G_{1}\left(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}^{n}\right)G_{2}\left(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}^{n}\right)\right]-{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[G_{1}\left(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}\right)G_{2}\left(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle+\left|{\mathbb{E}}\left[G_{1}\left(\epsilon^{n}\right)G_{2}\left(\epsilon^{n}\right)\right]-{\mathbb{E}}\left[G_{1}\left(\epsilon\right)G_{2}\left(\epsilon\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle+\left|{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[G_{1}\left(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}^{n}\right)G_{2}\left(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}^{n}\right)\right]-{\mathbb{E}}\left[G_{1}\left(\epsilon^{n}\right)G_{2}\left(\epsilon^{n}\right)\right]\right|,

which converges to zero due to (
A.5
), (
A.5
) and (
A.5
).
∎

Fix
1\leq i,j\leq p+1
and
k,l\in{\mathbb{N}}
. Let
G_{1}(f)=F_{i,k}(\pi_{i}f)
and
G_{2}(f)=F_{j,l}(\pi_{j}f)
for
f\in\left(L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])\right)^{p+1}
. Then
|G_{1}(f)|\leq\lVert f\rVert_{\left(L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])\right)^{p+1}}
implying
\lVert G_{1}\rVert\leq 1
and similarly
\lVert G_{2}\rVert\leq 1
. By Claim
A.2
, it follows that

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[F_{i,k}(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}(i))F_{j,l}(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}}(j))\right]={\mathbb{E}}_{O}\left[F_{i,k}(\epsilon_{O}(i))F_{j,l}(\epsilon_{O}(j))\right],

which in turn implies that

{\mathbb{E}}_{O}\left[F_{1:n}(\epsilon_{O})F_{1:n}(\epsilon_{O})^{T}\right]={\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[F_{1:n}(\epsilon_{A}^{\prime\prime})F_{1:n}(\epsilon_{A}^{\prime\prime})^{T}\right].

Step 7: Optimize over the shifts

Recall the definition of
\textbf{v}_{n}
from step 4. We utilize (
A.44
) when we now return to (
A.43
),

\displaystyle R_{A^{\prime\prime}}(\beta)

\displaystyle=\textbf{v}_{n}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[\left(F_{1:n}(A^{\prime\prime})+F_{1:n}(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}})\right)\left(F_{1:n}(A^{\prime\prime})+F_{1:n}(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}})\right)^{T}\right]\textbf{v}_{n}^{T}

\displaystyle=\lim_{n\to\infty}\textbf{v}_{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[F_{1:n}(A^{\prime\prime})F_{1:n}(A^{\prime\prime})^{T}\right]\textbf{v}_{n}^{T}+\lim_{n\to\infty}\textbf{v}_{n}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime\prime}}\left[F_{1:n}(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}})F_{1:n}(\epsilon_{A^{\prime\prime}})^{T}\right]\textbf{v}_{n}^{T}

\displaystyle=\lim_{n\to\infty}\textbf{v}_{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[F_{1:n}(A^{\prime\prime})F_{1:n}(A^{\prime\prime})^{T}\right]\textbf{v}_{n}^{T}+\lim_{n\to\infty}\textbf{v}_{n}{\mathbb{E}}_{O}\left[F_{1:n}(\epsilon_{O})F_{1:n}(\epsilon_{O})^{T}\right]\textbf{v}_{n}^{T}

and simlarly we have

\displaystyle R_{A}(\beta)=\lim_{n\to\infty}\textbf{v}_{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[F_{1:n}(A)F_{1:n}(A)^{T}\right]\textbf{v}_{n}^{T}+\lim_{n\to\infty}\textbf{v}_{n}{\mathbb{E}}_{O}\left[F_{1:n}(\epsilon_{O})F_{1:n}(\epsilon_{O})^{T}\right]\textbf{v}_{n}^{T}.

(A.48)

Take a sequence
\{A_{n}\}_{n\in{\mathbb{N}}}\subset C^{\gamma}_{\mathcal{A}}(A)
such that
\lim_{n\to\infty}R_{A_{n}}(\beta)=\sup_{A^{\prime}\in C^{\gamma}_{\mathcal{A}}(A)}R_{A^{\prime}}(\beta)
. Fix any
\Delta>0
. Let
\tilde{A}_{\Delta}\in C^{\gamma}_{\mathcal{A}}(A)
be such that
\lVert\tilde{A}_{\Delta}-\sqrt{\gamma}A\rVert_{\mathcal{V}}<\eta
where
\eta
is chosen such that
\left|R_{\tilde{A}_{\Delta}}(\beta)-R_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}(\beta)\right|<\Delta
, which is possible due to Lemma
3.1
and the fact that
\sqrt{\gamma}A\in\bar{\mathcal{A}}
. Define the sets

C_{m}=\{\tilde{A}_{\Delta}\}\cup\left(\bigcup_{k=1}^{m}\{A_{m}\}\right),m\in{\mathbb{N}}.

Fix
m\in{\mathbb{N}}
. Since there are only finitely many elements in
C_{m}
, we have that
\lim_{n\to\infty}\textbf{v}_{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[F_{1:n}(A^{\prime\prime})F_{1:n}(A^{\prime\prime})^{T}\right]\textbf{v}_{n}^{T}
uniformly over all
A^{\prime\prime}\in C_{m}
, so we may take
N\in{\mathbb{N}}
such that

\left|\lim_{n\to\infty}\textbf{v}_{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[F_{1:n}(A^{\prime\prime})F_{1:n}(A^{\prime\prime})^{T}\right]\textbf{v}_{n}^{T}-\textbf{v}_{N}{\mathbb{E}}\left[F_{1:N}(A^{\prime\prime})F_{1:N}(A^{\prime\prime})^{T}\right]\textbf{v}_{N}^{T}\right|<\Delta,\forall A^{\prime\prime}\in C_{m}

and

\left|\lim_{n\to\infty}\textbf{v}_{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[F_{1:n}(A)F_{1:n}(A)^{T}\right]\textbf{v}_{n}^{T}-\textbf{v}_{N}{\mathbb{E}}\left[F_{1:N}(A)F_{1:N}(A)^{T}\right]\textbf{v}_{N}^{T}\right|<\Delta.

Let
g_{i}(s)=\sum_{k=1}^{N}\textbf{v}_{N}((i-1)N+k)\phi_{i,k}(s)
for
1\leq i\leq p+1
. Then clearly
g_{i}\in L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])
. We will now utilize our results from step 2. Borrowing similar notation from step 2, note that

\displaystyle\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]^{2}}\left(g_{1}(s),\ldots,g_{p+1}(s)\right)K^{N}_{A^{\prime\prime}}(s,t)\left(g_{1}(t),\ldots,g_{p+1}(t)\right)^{T}dsdt

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{p+1}\sum_{j=1}^{p+1}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]^{2}}g_{i}(s)g_{j}(t)K^{N}_{(i,j)}(s,t)dsdt

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{p+1}\sum_{j=1}^{p+1}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]^{2}}g_{i}(s)g_{j}(t)K^{N}_{(j,i)}(s,t)dsdt

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{p+1}\sum_{j=1}^{p+1}\sum_{m=1}^{N}\sum_{v=1}^{N}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]^{2}}\textbf{v}_{N}((i-1)N+m)\phi_{i,m}(s){\mathbb{E}}\left[\sum_{k=1}^{N}\sum_{l=1}^{N}F_{i,k}(A^{\prime\prime}(i))F_{j,l}(A^{\prime\prime}(j))\phi_{i,k}(s)\phi_{j,l}(t)\right]\textbf{v}_{N}((j-1)N+v)\phi_{j,v}(s)dsdt

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{p+1}\sum_{j=1}^{p+1}\sum_{k=1}^{N}\sum_{l=1}^{N}\textbf{v}_{N}((i-1)N+k){\mathbb{E}}\left[F_{i,k}(A^{\prime\prime}(i))F_{j,l}(A^{\prime\prime}(j))\right]\textbf{v}_{N}((j-1)N+l)

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{N(p+1)}\sum_{l=1}^{N(p+1)}\textbf{v}_{N}(k){\mathbb{E}}\left[F_{1:N}(A^{\prime\prime})F_{1:N}(A^{\prime\prime})^{T}\right]_{k,l}\textbf{v}_{N}(l)=\textbf{v}_{N}{\mathbb{E}}\left[F_{1:N}(A^{\prime\prime})F_{1:N}(A^{\prime\prime})^{T}\right]\textbf{v}_{N}^{T},

(A.49)

where we utilized the symmetry of
K^{N}_{A^{\prime\prime}}
to swap
i
and
j
in the second equality, as well as the fact that

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[F_{1:N}(A^{\prime\prime})F_{1:N}(A^{\prime\prime})^{T}\right]_{k,l}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(F_{1:N}(A^{\prime\prime})F_{1:N}(A^{\prime\prime})^{T}\right)_{k,l}\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(F_{1:N}(A^{\prime\prime})F_{1:N}(A^{\prime\prime})^{T}\right)_{(i-1)N+k^{\prime},(j-1)N+l^{\prime}}\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[F_{i,k^{\prime}}(A^{\prime\prime}(i))F_{j,l^{\prime}}(A^{\prime\prime}(j))\right],

if
k=(i-1)N+k^{\prime}
and
l=(j-1)N+l^{\prime}
, for
1\leq i,j\leq p+1
and
1\leq k^{\prime},l^{\prime}\leq N
. By the definition of
C^{\gamma}_{\mathcal{A}}(A)
, (
A.5
) and the fact that
A^{\prime\prime}\in C_{m}\subset C^{\gamma}_{\mathcal{A}}(A)
we have,

\displaystyle\textbf{v}_{N}{\mathbb{E}}\left[F_{1:N}(A^{\prime\prime})F_{1:N}(A^{\prime\prime})^{T}\right]\textbf{v}_{N}^{T}

\displaystyle=\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(g_{1}(s),\ldots,g_{p+1}(s)\right)K^{N}_{A^{\prime\prime}}(s,t)\left(g_{1}(t),\ldots,g_{p+1}(t)\right)^{T}dsdt

\displaystyle=\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]^{2}}\left(g_{1}(s),\ldots,g_{p+1}(s)\right)K_{A^{\prime\prime}}(s,t)\left(g_{1}(t),\ldots,g_{p+1}(t)\right)^{T}dsdt

\displaystyle\leq\gamma\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]^{2}}\left(g_{1}(s),\ldots,g_{p+1}(s)\right)K_{A}(s,t)\left(g_{1}(t),\ldots,g_{p+1}(t)\right)^{T}dsdt

\displaystyle=\gamma\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]^{2}}\left(g_{1}(s),\ldots,g_{p+1}(s)\right)K^{N}_{A}(s,t)\left(g_{1}(t),\ldots,g_{p+1}(t)\right)^{T}dsdt

\displaystyle=\gamma\textbf{v}_{N}{\mathbb{E}}\left[F_{1:N}(A)F_{1:N}(A)^{T}\right]\textbf{v}_{N}^{T}

\displaystyle\leq\gamma\lim_{n\to\infty}\textbf{v}_{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[F_{1:n}(A)F_{1:n}(A)^{T}\right]\textbf{v}_{n}^{T}+\Delta

Since

\displaystyle\lim_{n\to\infty}\gamma\textbf{v}_{n}{\mathbb{E}}_{A}\left[\left(F_{1:n}(A)+F_{1:n}(\epsilon_{A})\right)\left(F_{1:n}(A)+F_{1:n}(\epsilon_{A})\right)^{T}\right]\textbf{v}_{n}^{T}=\gamma R_{A}(\beta)

and analogously

\displaystyle\lim_{n\to\infty}\textbf{v}_{n}{\mathbb{E}}_{O}\left[F(\epsilon_{O})F(\epsilon_{O})^{T}\right]\textbf{v}_{n}^{T}=R_{O}(\beta)

it therefore follows that

\displaystyle R_{A^{\prime\prime}}(\beta)\leq\gamma R_{A}(\beta)+(1-\gamma)R_{O}(\beta)+\Delta=\frac{1}{2}R_{+}(\beta)+\left(\gamma-\frac{1}{2}\right)R_{\Delta}(\beta)+\Delta.

Since
\tilde{A}_{\Delta}\in C_{m}
, for every
m\in{\mathbb{N}}
, we have

\displaystyle\max_{A^{\prime\prime}\in C_{m}}R_{A^{\prime\prime}}(\beta)

\displaystyle\geq R_{\tilde{A}_{\Delta}}(\beta)

\displaystyle\geq R_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}(\beta)-\Delta=\frac{1}{2}R_{+}(\beta)+\left(\gamma-\frac{1}{2}\right)R_{\Delta}(\beta)-\Delta.

Hence

\displaystyle\left|\max_{A^{\prime\prime}\in C_{m}}R_{A^{\prime\prime}}(\beta)-\left(\frac{1}{2}R_{+}(\beta)+\left(\gamma-\frac{1}{2}\right)R_{\Delta}(\beta)\right)\right|<\Delta.

Since
\lim_{n\to\infty}R_{A_{n}}(\beta)=\sup_{A^{\prime}\in C^{\gamma}_{\mathcal{A}}(A)}R_{A^{\prime}}(\beta)
, and
R_{A_{n}}(\beta)\leq\sup_{A^{\prime}\in C^{\gamma}_{\mathcal{A}}(A)}R_{A^{\prime}}(\beta)
(since
A_{n}\in C^{\gamma}_{\mathcal{A}}(A)
) it follows that
\lim_{m\to\infty}\max_{A^{\prime\prime}\in C_{m}}R_{A^{\prime\prime}}(\beta)=\sup_{A^{\prime}\in C^{\gamma}_{\mathcal{A}}(A)}R_{A^{\prime}}(\beta)
and therefore there exists
M\in{\mathbb{N}}
such that if
m\geq M
,
\left|\max_{A^{\prime\prime}\in C_{m}}R_{A^{\prime\prime}}(\beta)-\sup_{A^{\prime}\in C^{\gamma}_{\mathcal{A}}(A)}R_{A^{\prime}}(\beta)\right|<\Delta
.
Therefore, for
m\geq M

\displaystyle\left|\sup_{A^{\prime}\in C^{\gamma}_{\mathcal{A}}(A)}R_{A^{\prime}}(\beta)-\left(\frac{1}{2}R_{+}(\beta)+\left(\gamma-\frac{1}{2}\right)R_{\Delta}(\beta)\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\sup_{A^{\prime}\in C^{\gamma}_{\mathcal{A}}(A)}R_{A^{\prime}}(\beta)-\max_{A^{\prime\prime}\in C_{m}}R_{A^{\prime\prime}}(\beta)\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\max_{A^{\prime\prime}\in C_{m}}R_{A^{\prime\prime}}(\beta)-\left(\frac{1}{2}R_{+}(\beta)+\left(\gamma-\frac{1}{2}\right)R_{\Delta}(\beta)\right)\right|<2\Delta

and by letting
\Delta\to 0
we get

\sup_{A^{\prime}\in C^{\gamma}_{\mathcal{A}}(A)}R_{A^{\prime}}(\beta)=\frac{1}{2}R_{+}(\beta)+\left(\gamma-\frac{1}{2}\right)R_{\Delta}(\beta),

as was to be shown.
∎

A.6
Proof of Theorem
4.1

Proof.

In the proof of Theorem
3.7
we saw that,
\sup_{A^{\prime}\in C^{\gamma}_{\mathcal{A}}(A)}R_{A^{\prime}}(\beta)=R_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}(\beta)
. By the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality

\displaystyle\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]^{2}}K_{X^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}(i)X^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}(j)}(s,t)^{2}dsdt

\displaystyle=\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[X^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{s}(i)X^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{t}(j)\right]^{2}dsdt

\displaystyle\leq\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[\left(X_{s}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}(i)\right)^{2}\right]{\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[\left(X_{t}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}(j)\right)^{2}\right]dsdt

\displaystyle=\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}{\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[\left(X_{t}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}(i)\right)^{2}\right]dt\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}{\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[\left(X_{t}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}(j)\right)^{2}\right]ds,

which is finite by the assumption on
A
and
\epsilon
. Let
K^{i}
denote the i:th row of
K_{X^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}}
,
K^{i}_{t}=K^{i}(s,t)
and let
\{e_{n}\}_{n\in{\mathbb{N}}}
be an ON-basis for
L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p}
. Since

K_{X^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}(i)X^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}(j)}(s,t)^{2}\leq{\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[\left(X_{s}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}(i)\right)^{2}\right]{\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[\left(X_{t}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}(j)\right)^{2}\right]

and
{\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[\left(X_{t}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}(j)\right)^{2}\right]<\infty
a.e.
t
(since
\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}{\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[\left(X_{t}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}(j)\right)^{2}\right]dt<\infty
),
every component of
K^{i}_{t}
is a well-defined element of
L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])
for a.e.
t
. Therefore
K^{i}_{t}=\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\langle K_{t}^{i},e_{n}\rangle_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p}}e_{n}
and
\lVert K^{i}_{t}\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p}}^{2}=\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\langle K_{t}^{i},e_{n}\rangle_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p}}^{2}
. Next,

\displaystyle(\mathcal{K}e_{n})(t)=\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}K^{i}(s,t)e_{n}(s)ds=\left(\langle K_{t},e_{n}\rangle_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p}},\ldots,\langle K^{p}_{t},e_{n}\rangle_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p}}\right).

By monotone convergence,

\displaystyle\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\lVert\mathcal{K}e_{n}\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p}}^{2}=\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\langle K_{t}^{i},e_{n}\rangle_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p}}^{2}dt=\sum_{i=1}^{p}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\langle K_{t}^{i},e_{n}\rangle_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p}}^{2}dt.

This implies

\displaystyle\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\lVert\mathcal{K}e_{n}\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{p}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\lVert K^{i}_{t}\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p}}^{2}dt

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{j=1}^{p}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]^{2}}K_{X^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}(i)X^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}(j)}(s,t)^{2}dsdt<\infty.

It follows that
\mathcal{K}
is a Hilbert Schmidt operator and therefore compact on
L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p}
. Since
K_{X^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}}(s,t)=K_{X^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}}(t,s)
, this operator is also self-adjoint operator. By the Hilbert-Schmidt theorem,
\mathcal{K}
has an eigendecomposition,
\mathcal{K}=\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\alpha_{k}\langle\psi_{k},.\rangle\psi_{k}
, where its eigenfunctions,
\{\psi_{k}\}_{k}
, are orthonormal in
L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p}
. We then have that

\displaystyle K_{X^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}}(s,t)

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[\left(X^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{s}\right)^{T}X^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{t}\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[\mathcal{S}_{2:p}\left(\sqrt{\gamma}A+\epsilon_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\right)_{s}^{T}\mathcal{S}_{2:p}\left(\sqrt{\gamma}A+\epsilon_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\right)_{t}\right]

\displaystyle=\gamma{\mathbb{E}}\left[\mathcal{S}_{2:p}\left(A\right)_{s}^{T}\mathcal{S}_{2:p}\left(A\right)_{t}\right]+\sqrt{\gamma}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[\mathcal{S}_{2:p}\left(A\right)_{s}^{T}\mathcal{S}_{2:p}\left(\epsilon_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\right)_{t}\right]+{\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[\mathcal{S}_{2:p}\left(\epsilon_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\right)_{s}^{T}\mathcal{S}_{2:p}\left(A\right)_{t}\right]\right)

\displaystyle+{\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[\mathcal{S}_{2:p}\left(\epsilon_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\right)_{s}^{T}\mathcal{S}_{2:p}\left(\epsilon_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\right)_{t}\right]

Take some arbitrary basis of
L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})
, say the basis provided in the theorem statement,
\{\phi_{n}\}_{n\in{\mathbb{N}}}
, and let
V_{n}=\mathsf{span}\left(\{\phi_{m_{1}}\otimes\phi_{m_{2}}\}_{1\leq m_{1},m_{2}\leq n}\right)
and
f\in V_{n}
so that
f=\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}a_{k,l}\phi_{k}\otimes\phi_{l}
for some
\{a_{k,l}\}_{1\leq k,l\leq n}
. We have for
2\leq k,l\leq p+1
by Claim
A.1
,

\displaystyle\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[\mathcal{S}_{k}\left(\sqrt{\gamma}A\right)_{s}\mathcal{S}_{l}\left(\epsilon_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\right)_{t}\right]f(s,t)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}a_{k,l}{\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{k}\left(\sqrt{\gamma}A\right)_{s}\phi_{k}(s)ds\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\mathcal{S}_{l}\left(\epsilon_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\right)_{t}\phi_{l}(t)dt\right]=0.

Letting
P_{n}
denote projection on the space
V_{n}
, so that
\lVert P_{n}f-f\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}\to 0
it then follows that

\displaystyle\left|\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[\mathcal{S}_{k}\left(\sqrt{\gamma}A\right)_{s}\mathcal{S}_{l}\left(\epsilon_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\right)_{t}\right]f(s,t)-\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[\mathcal{S}_{k}\left(\sqrt{\gamma}A\right)_{s}\mathcal{S}_{l}\left(\epsilon_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\right)_{t}\right](P_{n}f)(s,t)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[\mathcal{S}_{k}\left(\sqrt{\gamma}A\right)_{s}\mathcal{S}_{l}\left(\epsilon_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\right)_{t}\right]^{2}dsdt\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\lVert P_{n}f-f\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[\mathcal{S}_{k}\left(\sqrt{\gamma}A\right)_{s}^{2}\right]{\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[\mathcal{S}_{l}\left(\epsilon_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\right)_{t}^{2}\right]dsdt\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\lVert P_{n}f-f\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}

\displaystyle\leq\gamma\lVert\mathcal{S}\left(A\right)\rVert_{\mathcal{V}}\lVert\mathcal{S}\left(\epsilon\right)\rVert_{\mathcal{V}}\lVert P_{n}f-f\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})},

which converges to zero. Since
g(s,t)={\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[\mathcal{S}_{k}\left(\sqrt{\gamma}A\right)_{s}\mathcal{S}_{l}\left(\epsilon_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\right)_{t}\right]\in L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})
and
\lVert gf\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}=0
,
\forall f\in L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})
this implies
g=0
a.e. in
[T_{1},T_{2}]^{2}
, implying
{\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[\mathcal{S}_{2:p}\left(\sqrt{\gamma}A\right)_{s}^{T}\mathcal{S}_{2:p}\left(\epsilon_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\right)_{t}\right]=0
a.e. in
[T_{1},T_{2}]^{2}
. Analogous arguments shows that

{\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[\mathcal{S}_{2:p}\left(\epsilon_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\right)_{s}^{T}\mathcal{S}_{2:p}\left(\sqrt{\gamma}A\right)_{t}\right]=0,

and that a.e. in
[T_{1},T_{2}]^{2}
,

{\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[\mathcal{S}_{2:p}\left(\epsilon_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\right)_{s}^{T}\mathcal{S}_{2:p}\left(\epsilon_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\right)_{t}\right]={\mathbb{E}}_{O}\left[\mathcal{S}_{2}\left(\epsilon_{O}\right)_{s}\mathcal{S}_{2}\left(\epsilon_{O}\right)_{t}\right].

We now prove that the formula (
4.11
) is valid given (
4.10
). If
\mathcal{K}
is injective then
W=\{0\}
, which will imply the uniqueness. Let
\mathcal{L}_{1}=\overline{\mathsf{span}}\left(\{\psi_{k}\}_{k}\right)
and
\mathcal{L}_{2}:=\mathcal{L}_{1}^{\perp}
. Then
\mathcal{L}_{2}
is a closed subspace of
L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p}
and is therefore separable, which implies it has a countable ON-basis,
\{\eta_{k}\}_{k}
and moreover
L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p}=\mathcal{L}_{1}\oplus\mathcal{L}_{2}
as well as
L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p}=\overline{\mathsf{span}}\left(\{\psi_{k}\}_{k}\cup\{\eta_{k}\}_{k}\right)
. Let
\{\tilde{\psi}_{k}\}_{k}
be an enumeration of the ON-system
\{\psi_{k}\}_{k}\cup\{\eta_{k}\}_{k}
, which is a basis for
L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p}
. We will now show that
\{\tilde{\psi}_{l}\otimes\phi_{k}\}_{k,l\in{\mathbb{N}}}
is an ON-basis for
L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})^{p}
. Denote the components of
\tilde{\psi}_{l}
as
\tilde{\psi}_{l}=\left(\tilde{\psi}_{l,1},\ldots,\tilde{\psi}_{l,p}\right)
, where
\tilde{\psi}_{l,i}\in L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])
. Then

\displaystyle\langle\tilde{\psi}_{l_{1}}\otimes\phi_{k_{1}},\tilde{\psi}_{l_{2}}\otimes\phi_{k_{2}}\rangle_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})^{p}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{p}\langle\tilde{\psi}_{l,i}\otimes\phi_{k_{1}},\tilde{\psi}_{l_{2},i}\otimes\phi_{k_{2}}\rangle_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{p}\langle\tilde{\psi}_{l_{1},i},\tilde{\psi}_{l_{2},i}\rangle_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}\langle\phi_{k_{1}},\phi_{k_{2}}\rangle_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}

\displaystyle=\langle\tilde{\psi}_{l_{1}},\tilde{\psi}_{l_{2}}\rangle_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p}}\langle\phi_{k_{1}},\phi_{k_{2}}\rangle_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}=\delta_{l_{1},l_{2}}\delta_{k_{1},k_{2}},

which shows the orthogonality. For the completeness of this basis, let
f\in\left(\mathsf{span}\{\tilde{\psi}_{l}\otimes\phi_{k}\}_{k,l\in{\mathbb{N}}}\right)^{\perp}
so that

\langle f,\tilde{\psi}_{l}\otimes\phi_{k}\rangle_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})^{p}}=0,

for all
k,l\in{\mathbb{N}}
. Explicitly this means,

\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}f(t,\tau)\tilde{\psi}_{l}(\tau)d\tau\right)\phi_{k}(t)dt=0.

If we let
g_{l}(t)=\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}f(t,\tau)\tilde{\psi}_{l}(\tau)d\tau
then, by the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality (applied component-wise),
g_{l}\in L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])
. Moreover,
\langle g,\phi_{k}\rangle_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}=0
,
\forall k\in{\mathbb{N}}
implying

g_{l}=\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\langle g_{l},\phi_{k}\rangle_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}\phi_{k}=0,

in
L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])
, further implying that
g_{l}=0
a.e. in
[T_{1},T_{2}]
. Let
F_{l}=\{t:g_{l}(t)\not=0\}
and
F=\bigcup_{l\in{\mathbb{N}}}F_{l}
. On
F^{c}
,

\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}f(t,\tau)\tilde{\psi}_{l}(\tau)d\tau=0,\forall l\in{\mathbb{N}},

implying
f(t,\tau)=0
outside a zero-set
F^{\prime}\in[T_{1},T_{2}]
. By the Fubini theorem,

\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}|f(t,\tau)|d\tau dt=\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]\setminus F}\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]\setminus F^{\prime}}|f(t,\tau)|d\tau\right)dt=0.

This implies that
f=0
a.e. in
[T_{1},T_{2}]^{2}
, which leads to the conclusion that
\left(\mathsf{span}\{\tilde{\psi}_{l}\otimes\phi_{k}\}_{k,l\in{\mathbb{N}}}\right)^{\perp}=\{0\}
, implying that
\{\tilde{\psi}_{l}\otimes\phi_{k}\}_{k,l\in{\mathbb{N}}}
is indeed a basis. We therefore have the following representation,

\displaystyle L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})

\displaystyle=\left\{\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\lambda_{k,l}\tilde{\psi}_{k}\otimes\phi_{l}:\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\lambda_{k,l}^{2}<\infty\right\}

\displaystyle=\left\{\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\lambda_{k,l}^{(1)}\psi_{k}\otimes\phi_{l}+\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\lambda_{k,l}^{(2)}\eta_{k}\otimes\phi_{l}:\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}(\lambda_{k,l}^{(1)})^{2}+\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}(\lambda_{k,l}^{(2)})^{2}<\infty\right\}.

Moreover
X^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\in L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])
a.s. and hence if we let
S_{n}(t,\omega)=\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(\chi^{(1)}_{k}(\omega)\psi_{k}(t)+\chi^{(2)}_{k}(\omega)\eta_{k}(t)\right)
then
S_{n}\xrightarrow{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}X^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}
a.s.. Note however that

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(\chi^{(2)}_{l}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\eta_{l}(s)\left(X_{s}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\right)^{T}X_{t}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\eta_{l}(t)^{T}dtds\right]

\displaystyle=\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\eta_{l}(s){\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(X_{s}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\right)^{T}X_{t}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\right]\eta_{l}(t)^{T}dtds

\displaystyle=\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\eta_{l}(s)K_{X^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}}(t,s)\eta_{l}(t)^{T}dtds

\displaystyle=\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(\mathcal{K}\eta_{l})(t)\eta_{l}(t)^{T}dtds

\displaystyle=\lim_{N\to\infty}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\alpha_{n}\langle\psi_{n},\eta_{l}\rangle_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p}}\psi_{n}(t)\eta_{l}(t)^{T}dt

\displaystyle=\lim_{N\to\infty}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\alpha_{n}\langle\psi_{n},\eta_{l}\rangle_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p}}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\psi_{n}(t)\eta_{l}(t)dt=0.

This implies that in fact
S_{n}(t,\omega)=\sum_{k=1}^{n}\chi^{(1)}_{k}(\omega)\psi_{k}(t)\xrightarrow{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}X^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}
a.s.. We will therefore denote
\chi_{l}=\chi^{(1)}_{l}
from now on. Since we expand
X^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}
in the basis given by the eigenfunctions of
K_{X^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}}
we also have that the sequence
\{\chi_{l}\}_{l}
is orthogonal,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\chi_{l_{1}}\chi_{l_{2}}\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\psi_{l_{1}}(s)\left(X_{s}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\right)^{T}X_{t}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\psi_{l_{2}}(t)^{T}dtds\right]

\displaystyle=\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\psi_{l_{1}}(s){\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(X_{s}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\right)^{T}X_{t}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\right]\psi_{l_{2}}(t)^{T}dtds

\displaystyle=\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\psi_{l_{1}}(s)K_{X^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}}(t,s)\psi_{l_{2}}(t)^{T}dtds

\displaystyle=\lim_{N\to\infty}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\alpha_{n}\langle\psi_{n},\psi_{l_{1}}\rangle_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p}}\psi_{n}(t)\psi_{l_{2}}(t)dtds

\displaystyle=\lim_{N\to\infty}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\alpha_{n}\langle\psi_{n},\psi_{l_{1}}\rangle_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p}}\langle\psi_{n},\psi_{l_{2}}\rangle_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p}}

\displaystyle=\lim_{N\to\infty}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\alpha_{n}\delta_{n,l_{1}}\delta_{n,l_{2}}=\alpha_{l_{1}}\delta_{l_{1},l_{2}}.

Let

V=\left\{\int\beta(t,\tau)X^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{\tau}d\tau:\beta\in L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})^{p}\right\}.

Setting
\delta=\inf_{\beta\in L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})^{p}}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(Y_{t}-\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\beta(t,\tau)X^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{\tau}d\tau\right)^{2}\right]dt
, it follows that there exists a sequence
\{\beta_{n}\}_{n}\in L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})^{p}
such that
\delta=\lim_{n\to\infty}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(Y_{t}-\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\beta_{n}(t,\tau)X^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{\tau}d\tau\right)^{2}\right]dt
. If we let
W_{n}(t)=\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\beta_{n}(t,\tau)X^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{\tau}d\tau
then
\{W_{n}\}_{n}\subset V
and
\delta=\lim_{n\to\infty}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(Y_{t}-W_{n}(t)\right)^{2}\right]dt
which implies that
\inf_{W\in V}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(Y_{t}-W(t)\right)^{2}\right]dt\leq\delta
. Therefore

\displaystyle\inf_{\beta\in L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})^{p}}\int{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(Y_{t}-\int\beta(t,\tau)X^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{\tau}d\tau\right)^{2}\right]dt

\displaystyle\geq\inf_{W\in V}\int{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(Y_{t}-W(t)\right)^{2}\right]dt

\displaystyle\geq\inf_{W\in\bar{V}}\int{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(Y_{t}-W(t)\right)^{2}\right]dt.

(A.50)

This implies that if
\arg\min_{W\in\bar{V}}\int{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(Y_{t}-W(t)\right)^{2}\right]dt\in V
then there exists
\tilde{\beta}\in L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})^{p}
such that

\arg\min_{W\in\bar{V}}\int{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(Y_{t}-W(t)\right)^{2}\right]dt=\int\tilde{\beta}(t,\tau)X^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{\tau}d\tau
and

\arg\min_{\beta\in L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})^{p}}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(Y_{t}-\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\beta(t,\tau)X^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}(\tau)d\tau\right)^{2}\right]dt=\tilde{\beta}.

Consider an arbitrary element in
V
,

\displaystyle h(t)=\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\beta(t,\tau)X^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{\tau}d\tau.

Let

S_{n}^{\beta}(t,\tau)=\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\lambda_{(1),k,l}^{\beta}\psi_{l}(\tau)\phi_{k}(t)+\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\lambda_{(2),k,l}^{\beta}\eta_{l}(\tau)\phi_{k}(t),

then
S_{n}^{\beta}\xrightarrow{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})^{p}}\beta
and
\lVert S_{n}^{\beta}\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})^{p}}=\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\left(\left(\lambda_{(1),k,l}^{\beta}\right)^{2}+\left(\lambda_{(2),k,l}^{\beta}\right)^{2}\right)
.
Similarly to the proof of Theorem
3.7
,

\displaystyle\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(\beta(t,\tau))X^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{\tau}d\tau\right)^{2}dt

\displaystyle=\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{p}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(\beta(t,\tau))(i)X^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{\tau}(i)d\tau\right)^{2}dt

\displaystyle\leq 2^{p}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left|X^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{\tau}(i)\right|^{2}d\tau\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left|(\beta(t,\tau))(i)\right|^{2}d\tau dt,

Therefore if we let
Q(t)=\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(\beta(t,\tau))X^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{\tau}d\tau
and

S_{N}^{\int}(t)=\sum_{n=1}^{N}\left\langle\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\beta(.,\tau)X^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{\tau}d\tau,\phi_{k}\right\rangle_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}\phi_{k}(t)

Since

\displaystyle\left\langle\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\beta(.,\tau)X^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{\tau}d\tau,\phi_{k}\right\rangle_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}

\displaystyle=\lim_{n\to\infty}\sum_{k^{\prime}=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}\lambda_{(1)k,l}^{\beta}\chi_{m}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\psi_{l}(\tau)^{T}\psi_{m}(\tau)\phi_{k^{\prime}}(t)\phi_{k}(t)d\tau dt

\displaystyle+\lim_{n\to\infty}\sum_{k^{\prime}=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}\lambda_{(2)k,l}^{\beta}\chi_{m}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\eta_{l}(\tau)^{T}\psi_{m}(\tau)\phi_{k^{\prime}}(t)\phi_{k}(t)d\tau dt

\displaystyle=\lim_{n\to\infty}\sum_{k^{\prime}=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}\lambda_{(1),k,l}^{\beta}\chi_{m}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\delta_{l,m}\delta_{k^{\prime},k}=\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\lambda_{(1)k,l}^{\beta}\chi_{l}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}

we get,

\displaystyle S_{n}^{\int}(t)

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\lambda_{(1),k,l}^{\beta}\chi_{l}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\phi_{k}(t).

Arguing as in the proof of Theorem
3.7
we get
\lim_{n\to\infty}{\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(S_{n}^{\int}(t)-Q(t)\right)^{2}dt\right]=0
and then

\displaystyle\lim_{n\to\infty}{\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\lambda^{\beta}_{(1),k,l}\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}\phi_{k}(t)-\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\beta(t,\tau)X^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{\tau}d\tau\right)^{2}dt\right]=0.

(A.51)

This implies that

\displaystyle\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\beta(t,\tau)X^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{\tau}d\tau=\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\lambda^{(1)}_{k,l}\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}\phi_{k}(t),

where the limit exists in
L^{2}(dt\times{\mathbb{P}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A})
. This implies that we may rewrite
V
as,

\displaystyle V=\left\{\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\lambda_{k,l}\phi_{k}(t)\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}:\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\lambda_{k,l}^{2}<\infty\right\},

(A.52)

where the series
\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\lambda_{k,l}\phi_{k}(t)\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}
converges in
L^{2}(dt\times{\mathbb{P}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A})
. Let us now show that
\bar{V}=\overline{\mathsf{span}}\left\{\phi_{k}\frac{\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}}{\|\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{P}})}}\right\}_{k,l\in{\mathbb{N}}}
. First note that for an arbitrary
h\in V
,

\displaystyle h=\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\lambda_{k,l}\phi_{k}(t)\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}(\omega)=\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\lambda_{k,l}\lVert\chi_{l}\rVert_{L^{2}({\mathbb{P}})}\frac{\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}}{\|\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{P}})}}\phi_{k}(t),

which means that if
\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\lambda_{k,l}^{2}\|\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{P}})}^{2}<\infty
, then
h\in\overline{\mathsf{span}}\left\{\phi_{k}(t)\frac{\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}}{\|\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{P}})}}\right\}_{k,l\in{\mathbb{N}}}
. Define
\beta_{l}=\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\lambda_{k,l}^{2}
and note that
\{\beta_{l}\}_{l\in{\mathbb{N}}}\in\mathit{l}^{1}\subset\mathit{l}^{2}
. Since
\overline{\mathsf{span}}\left\{\phi_{k}(t)\frac{\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}}{\|\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{P}})}}\right\}_{k,l\in{\mathbb{N}}}
is ON,

\overline{\mathsf{span}}\left\{\phi_{k}(t)\frac{\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}}{\|\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{P}})}}\right\}_{k,l\in{\mathbb{N}}}=\left\{\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\lambda_{k,l}\phi_{k}(t)\frac{\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}}{\|\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{P}})}}:\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\lambda_{k,l}^{2}<\infty\right\}

and by the Cauchy-Schwartz inequality

\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\lambda_{k,l}^{2}\|\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{P}})}^{2}=\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\beta_{l}\|\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{P}})}^{2}\leq\sqrt{\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\beta_{l}^{2}}\sqrt{\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\|\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{P}})}^{4}}<\infty.

Hence
V\subset\overline{\mathsf{span}}\left\{\phi_{k}(t)\frac{\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}}{\|\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{P}})}}\right\}_{k,l\in{\mathbb{N}}}
, taking closure yields
\overline{V}\subset\overline{\mathsf{span}}\left\{\phi_{k}(t)\frac{\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}}{\|\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{P}})}}\right\}_{k,l\in{\mathbb{N}}}
. Take

g\in\mathsf{span}\left\{\phi_{k}(t)\frac{\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}}{\|\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{P}})}}\right\}_{k,l\in{\mathbb{N}}}=\left\{\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{l=1}^{L}\lambda_{k,l}\phi_{k}(t)\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}(\omega):K,L\in{\mathbb{N}},\lambda_{k,l}\in{\mathbb{R}}\right\}.

Clearly
g\in V
, i.e.
\mathsf{span}\left\{\phi_{k}(t)\frac{\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}}{\|\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{P}})}}\right\}_{k,l\in{\mathbb{N}}}\subset V
and taking closure on both sides shows
\overline{\mathsf{span}}\left\{\phi_{k}(t)\frac{\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}}{\|\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{P}})}}\right\}_{k,l\in{\mathbb{N}}}\subset\bar{V}
, thus
\overline{\mathsf{span}}\left\{\phi_{k}(t)\frac{\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}}{\|\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{P}})}}\right\}_{k,l\in{\mathbb{N}}}=\bar{V}
. Recall that
Y^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{t}=\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}Z^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{k}\phi_{k}(t)
. Since
\bar{V}
is a closed subspace of
L^{2}({\mathbb{P}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\times dt)
which is a Hilbert space, we have by the Hilbert space projection theorem that

\arg\min_{W\in\bar{V}}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}{\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[\left(Y^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{t}-W_{t}\right)^{2}\right]dt=Proj_{\bar{V}}(Y^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}).

As
\bar{V}=\overline{\mathsf{span}}\left\{\phi_{k}(t)\frac{\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}}{\|\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{P}})}}\right\}_{k,l\in{\mathbb{N}}}
, it follows that
\left\{\phi_{k}(t)\frac{\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}}{\|\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{P}})}}\right\}_{k,l\in{\mathbb{N}}}
is an ON-basis for this space and therefore

\displaystyle Proj_{\bar{V}}(Y^{\sqrt{\gamma}A})

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\langle Y,\phi_{k}\frac{\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}}{\|\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{P}})}}\rangle_{H}\phi_{k}\frac{\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}}{\|\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{P}})}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\phi_{k}(t)\frac{\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}(\omega)}{\|\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{P}})}^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\int Y_{\tau}\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}\phi_{k}(\tau)d\tau\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\phi_{k}(t)\frac{\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}(\omega)}{\|\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{P}})}^{2}}\int{\mathbb{E}}\left[Y_{\tau}\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}\right]\phi_{k}(\tau)d\tau

(A.53)

Since
S_{n}^{Y}\xrightarrow{L^{2}({\mathbb{P}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\times dt)}Y^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}

\displaystyle\|Y^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\phi_{k}\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}-S_{n}^{Y^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}}\phi_{k}\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{P}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\times dt)}

\displaystyle\leq\|Y^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}-S_{n}^{Y^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}}\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{P}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\times dt)}\|\phi_{k}\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{P}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\times dt)}

\displaystyle=\|Y^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}-S_{n}^{Y^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}}\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{P}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\times dt)}\|\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{P}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\times dt)},

which converges to zero for every
l
. Therefore

\displaystyle\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}{\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[Y^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{\tau}\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}\right]\phi_{k}(\tau)d\tau

\displaystyle=\sum_{m=1}^{\infty}{\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[Z^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{m}\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}\right]\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\phi_{m}(\tau)\phi_{k}(\tau)d\tau

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[Z^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{k}\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}\right],

which we then plug back into (
A.6
) to conclude that

\displaystyle Proj_{\bar{V}}(Y)=\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\phi_{k}(t)\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}\frac{{\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[Z_{k}\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[\left(\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}\right)^{2}\right]}.

(A.54)

If we let
\lambda_{k,l}=\frac{{\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[Z_{k}\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[\left(\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}\right)^{2}\right]}
then by assumption

\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\lambda_{k,l}^{2}<\infty
.
This implies that
Proj_{\bar{V}}(Y)\in V
, so the last inequality in (
A.6
) is in fact an equality attained at this projection. By representing
\beta\in L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})
as
\beta(t,\tau)=\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\lambda_{k,l}\phi_{k}(t)\psi_{k}(\tau)
we get

\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}B(t,\tau)X(\tau)d\tau=\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\lambda_{k,l}\phi_{k}(t)\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}(\omega).

Comparing with (
A.54
), we see that a solution for the
\arg\min
is to let
\lambda_{k,l}=\frac{{\mathbb{E}}\left[Z_{k}\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}\right)^{2}\right]}
, which yields
\beta(t,\tau)=\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\phi_{k}(t)\psi_{l}(\tau)\frac{{\mathbb{E}}\left[Z_{k}\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(\chi^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}_{l}\right)^{2}\right]}
. As
\{\phi_{k}\otimes\psi_{l}\}_{k,l\in{\mathbb{N}}}
(since
W=\{0\}
) is an ON basis for
L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})
, this element is unique.

On the other hand, suppose a unique
\arg\min
solution exists, then there must exist a unique
B\in L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})
implying that
\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}B(t,\tau)X(\tau)d\tau\in V
. We may represent
B=\sum_{k,l\in{\mathbb{N}}}\lambda_{k,l}\phi_{k}\otimes\psi_{l}
. Suppose
\exists k^{\prime},l^{\prime}\in{\mathbb{N}}
such that
\lambda_{k^{\prime},l^{\prime}}\not=\frac{{\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[Z_{k}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\chi_{l}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[\left(\chi_{l}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\right)^{2}\right]}
. Suppose first that
{\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[\left(\chi_{l^{\prime}}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\right)^{2}\right]=0
, implying that
\chi_{l^{\prime}}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}=0
,
{\mathbb{P}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}
a.s.. Then for any real number
\lambda\not=\lambda_{k,l}
, we have that if we set
\tilde{B}=\sum_{k,l\in{\mathbb{N}}}\tilde{\lambda}_{k,l}\phi_{k}\otimes\psi_{l}
, where
\tilde{\lambda}_{k,l}=\lambda_{k,l}
if
(k,l)\not=(k^{\prime},l^{\prime})
and
\tilde{\lambda}_{k^{\prime},l^{\prime}}=\lambda
then
\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\tilde{B}(t,\tau)X(\tau)d\tau=\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}B(t,\tau)X(\tau)d\tau
, implying that also
\tilde{B}
is a minimizer, contradicting the uniqueness of
B
. Suppose instead that
{\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[\left(\chi_{l^{\prime}}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\right)^{2}\right]\not=0
and let

\displaystyle f_{n}(\lambda)

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{k^{\prime}-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[\left(Z_{k}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}-\sum_{l=1}^{n}\lambda_{k,l}\chi_{l}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\right)^{2}\right]+{\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[\left(Z_{k^{\prime}}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}-\lambda\chi_{l^{\prime}}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+{\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[\left(Z_{k^{\prime}}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}-\sum_{1\leq l\leq n,l\not=l^{\prime}}\lambda_{k^{\prime},l}\chi_{l}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\right)^{2}\right]+\sum_{k=k^{\prime}+1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[\left(Z_{k}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}-\sum_{l=1}^{n}\lambda_{k,l}\chi_{l}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\right)^{2}\right]

then for any
n\geq k^{\prime}\vee l^{\prime}
,
\arg\min_{\lambda}f_{n}(\lambda)=\frac{{\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[Z_{k}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\chi_{l}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[\left(\chi_{l}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\right)^{2}\right]}
. If we now set
\tilde{\lambda}_{k,l}=\lambda_{k,l}
if
(k,l)\not=(k^{\prime},l^{\prime})
and
\tilde{\lambda}_{k^{\prime},l^{\prime}}=\frac{{\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[Z_{k}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\chi_{l}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[\left(\chi_{l}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\right)^{2}\right]}
implying that for every
n\geq k^{\prime}\vee l^{\prime}

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[\left(Z_{k}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}-\sum_{l=1}^{n}\tilde{\lambda}_{k,l}\chi_{l}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\right)^{2}\right]\leq\sum_{k=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[\left(Z_{k}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}-\sum_{l=1}^{n}\lambda_{k,l}\chi_{l}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\right)^{2}\right].

(A.55)

Define
\tilde{B}=\sum_{k,l\in{\mathbb{N}}}\tilde{\lambda}_{k,l}\phi_{k}\otimes\psi_{l}
. An argument analogous used to establish (
A.5
), shows that

R_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}(B)=\lim_{n\to\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[\left(Z_{k}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}-\sum_{l=1}^{n}\lambda_{k,l}\chi_{l}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\right)^{2}\right]

and

R_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}(\tilde{B})=\lim_{n\to\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[\left(Z_{k}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}-\sum_{l=1}^{n}\tilde{\lambda}_{k,l}\chi_{l}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\right)^{2}\right].

By letting
n\to\infty
in (
A.55
) we then obtain

R_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left(\tilde{B}\right)\leq R_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left(B\right),

which directly contradicts the uniqueness of the minimizer
B
. We also need to establish that in this case
\mathcal{K}
is injective. Suppose it is not, then
W\not=0
and in particular
\beta^{\prime}=\phi\otimes\eta\in W
. Noting that

\displaystyle\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\beta^{\prime}(t,\tau)X_{\tau}d\tau=\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\phi(t)\chi_{l}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\eta_{1}(\tau)^{T}\psi_{l}(\tau)d\tau=0.

Hence, if
\beta
is a solution to (
A.6
) then so is
\beta+\beta^{\prime}
, contradicting the uniqueness.

We now note that
Y^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}=\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(\sqrt{\gamma}A+\epsilon_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\right)
and
X^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}=\mathcal{S}_{2:p}\left(\sqrt{\gamma}A+\epsilon_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\right)
. Therefore, if we let
F_{k}(U)=\langle U,\psi_{k}\rangle_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p}}
, for
U\in L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p}
,
\overline{F}_{k}(u)=\langle u,\phi_{k}\rangle_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}
, for
u\in L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])
, and
S_{2:p}(.)=\left(S_{2}(.),\ldots,S_{p}(.)\right)
then

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[Z_{k}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\chi_{l}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\right]={\mathbb{E}}_{A}\left[\overline{F}_{k}\left(\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(\sqrt{\gamma}A+\epsilon_{A}\right)\right)F_{l}\left(\mathcal{S}_{2:p}\left(\sqrt{\gamma}A+\epsilon_{A}\right)\right)\right]

(A.56)

and

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[\left(\chi_{l}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\right)^{2}\right]={\mathbb{E}}\left[F_{l}\left(\mathcal{S}_{2:p}\left(\sqrt{\gamma}A+\epsilon_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\right)\right)^{2}\right].

(A.57)

Expanding (
A.56
) and utilizing Claim
A.1
yields

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[Z_{k}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\chi_{l}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\right]

\displaystyle=\gamma{\mathbb{E}}_{A}\left[\overline{F}_{k}\left(\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(A\right)\right)F_{l}\left(\mathcal{S}_{2:p}\left(A\right)\right)\right]+{\mathbb{E}}_{A}\left[\overline{F}_{k}\left(\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(\epsilon_{A}\right)\right)F_{l}\left(\mathcal{S}_{2:p}\left(\epsilon_{A}\right)\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\gamma{\mathbb{E}}_{A}\left[\overline{F}_{k}\left(\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(A+\epsilon_{A}\right)\right)F_{l}\left(\mathcal{S}_{2:p}\left(A+\epsilon_{A}\right)\right)\right]+\left(1-\gamma\right){\mathbb{E}}_{A}\left[\overline{F}_{k}\left(\mathcal{S}_{1}\left(\epsilon_{A}\right)\right)F_{l}\left(\mathcal{S}_{2:p}\left(\epsilon_{A}\right)\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\gamma{\mathbb{E}}_{A}\left[Z_{k}^{A}\chi_{l}^{A}\right]+(1-\gamma){\mathbb{E}}_{O}\left[Z_{k}^{O}\chi_{l}^{O}\right].

(A.58)

While expanding (
A.57
) and utilizing Claim
A.2
yields

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[\left(\chi_{l}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\gamma{\mathbb{E}}\left[F_{l}\left(\mathcal{S}_{2:p}\left(A\right)\right)^{2}\right]+{\mathbb{E}}\left[F_{l}\left(\mathcal{S}_{2:p}\left(\epsilon\right)\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\gamma{\mathbb{E}}\left[F_{l}\left(\mathcal{S}_{2:p}\left(A+\epsilon_{A}\right)\right)^{2}\right]+(1-\gamma){\mathbb{E}}\left[F_{l}\left(\mathcal{S}_{2:p}\left(\epsilon\right)\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\gamma{\mathbb{E}}_{A}\left[\left(\chi_{l}^{A}\right)^{2}\right]+(1-\gamma){\mathbb{E}}_{O}\left[\left(\chi_{l}^{O}\right)^{2}\right].

(A.59)

Therefore

\beta(t,\tau)=\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\frac{\gamma{\mathbb{E}}_{A}\left[Z_{k}^{A}\chi_{l}^{A}\right]+(1-\gamma){\mathbb{E}}_{O}\left[Z_{k}^{O}\chi_{l}^{O}\right]}{\gamma{\mathbb{E}}_{A}\left[\left(\chi_{l}^{A}\right)^{2}\right]+(1-\gamma){\mathbb{E}}_{O}\left[\left(\chi_{l}^{O}\right)^{2}\right]}\phi_{k}(t)\psi_{l}(\tau)

∎

A.7
Proof of Theorem
4.3

Proof.

By (
A.6
),

{\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[Z_{k}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}F_{1:n}\left(X^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\right)\right]=\gamma{\mathbb{E}}_{A}\left[Z_{k}^{A}F_{1:n}\left(X^{A}\right)\right]+(1-\gamma){\mathbb{E}}_{O}\left[Z_{k}^{O}F_{1:n}\left(X^{O}\right)\right]

and similar arguments imply

{\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[F_{1:n}\left(X^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\right)^{T}F_{1:n}\left(X^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\right)\right]=\sqrt{\gamma}{\mathbb{E}}_{A}\left[F_{1:n}\left(X^{A}\right)^{T}F_{1:n}\left(X^{A}\right)\right]+(1-\sqrt{\gamma}){\mathbb{E}}_{O}\left[F_{1:n}\left(X^{O}\right)^{T}F_{1:n}\left(X^{O}\right)\right].

Let
V_{n}=span\left(\mathcal{H}^{p+1}\left(\{\phi_{i,k}\}_{k\leq n,1\leq i\leq p+1}\right)\right)
. By assumption there exists an
N\in{\mathbb{N}}
such that if
n\geq N
then
\det\left(G_{n}\right)\not=0
. For
\{\lambda_{i,k,l}\}_{1\leq i\leq p,1\leq k,l\leq n}
, let

\displaystyle h_{n}\left(\lambda_{1,1,1}^{\prime},\ldots,\lambda_{p,n,n}^{\prime}\right)

\displaystyle=R_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left(\left(\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\lambda_{1,k,l}\phi_{k}\otimes\phi_{l},\ldots,\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\lambda_{p,k,l}\phi_{k}\otimes\phi_{l}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[\left(Z_{k}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\lambda_{i,k,l}^{\prime}\chi_{l}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}(i)\right)^{2}\right]+\sum_{k=n+1}^{\infty}{\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[\left(Z_{k}^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\right)^{2}\right].

Any minimum of
h_{n}
must fulfil
\nabla h_{n}=0
. Solving
\nabla h_{n}=0
gives,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[F_{1:n}\left(X^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\right)^{T}F_{1:n}\left(X^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\right)\right]\left(\lambda_{1,k,1}^{\prime},\ldots,\lambda_{1,k,n}^{\prime},\ldots,\lambda_{p,k,n}^{\prime}\right)={\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[Z_{k}F_{1:n}\left(X^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\right)\right],

for
1\leq k\leq n
. If
n\geq N
we therefore have the unique solution

\displaystyle\left(\lambda_{1,k,1}(n),\ldots,\lambda_{1,k,n}(n),\ldots,\lambda_{p,k,n}(n)\right)=G_{n}^{-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left[Z_{k}F_{1:n}\left(X^{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\right)\right].

Therefore
\beta_{n}
is the unique minimizer on
V_{n}
, implying that

\displaystyle R_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}(\beta_{n})\leq R_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}(P_{V_{n}}\beta)

(A.60)

for any
\beta\in L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})^{p}

Claim A.3
.

For
\{\tilde{\beta}_{n}\}_{n\in{\mathbb{N}}}\subset L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})^{p}
, if
\lVert\tilde{\beta}_{n}-\tilde{\beta}\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})^{p}}\to 0
for some
\tilde{\beta}\in L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})^{p}
then
R_{A^{\prime}}\left(\tilde{\beta}_{n}\right)\to R_{A^{\prime}}\left(\tilde{\beta}\right)
for any
A^{\prime}\in\mathcal{V}
.

Proof.

By the Cacuhy-Schwarz inequality

\displaystyle\left|R_{A^{\prime}}\left(\tilde{\beta}_{n}\right)-R_{A^{\prime}}\left(\tilde{\beta}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=1}^{p}2{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]^{2}}\left|Y^{A^{\prime}}_{t}X^{A^{\prime}}_{\tau}(i)\left((\tilde{\beta}_{n}(i))(t,\tau)-(\tilde{\beta}(i))(t,\tau)\right)\right|dtd\tau\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left|\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(\tilde{\beta}_{n}(i))(t,\tau)-(\tilde{\beta}(i))(t,\tau)\right)X^{A^{\prime}}_{\tau}(i)d\tau\right)\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(\tilde{\beta}_{n}(i))(t,\tau)+(\tilde{\beta}(i))(t,\tau)\right)X^{A^{\prime}}_{\tau}(i)d\tau\right)\right|dt\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=1}^{p}2\lVert\tilde{\beta}_{n}(i)-\tilde{\beta}(i)\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]^{2}}\left(Y^{A^{\prime}}_{t}\right)^{2}\left(X^{A^{\prime}}_{\tau}(i)\right)^{2}dtd\tau\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(\tilde{\beta}_{n}(i))(t,\tau)-(\tilde{\beta}(i))(t,\tau)\right)X^{A^{\prime}}_{\tau}(i)d\tau\right)^{2}dt\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(\tilde{\beta}_{n}(i))(t,\tau)+(\tilde{\beta}(i))(t,\tau)\right)X^{A^{\prime}}_{\tau}(i)d\tau\right)^{2}dt\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=1}^{p}2\lVert\tilde{\beta}_{n}(i)-\tilde{\beta}(i)\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}T{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\lVert Y^{A^{\prime}}\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}\lVert X^{A^{\prime}}_{\tau}(i)\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{p}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(\tilde{\beta}_{n}(i))(t,\tau)-(\tilde{\beta}(i))(t,\tau)\right)^{2}d\tau\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(X^{A^{\prime}}_{\tau}(i)\right)^{2}d\tau\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}dt\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(\tilde{\beta}_{n}(i))(t,\tau)+(\tilde{\beta}(i))(t,\tau)\right)^{2}d\tau\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(X^{A^{\prime}}_{\tau}(i)\right)^{2}d\tau\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}dt\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2T\lVert\tilde{\beta}_{n}-\tilde{\beta}\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})^{p}}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\lVert Y^{A^{\prime}}\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\lVert X^{A^{\prime}}\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})^{p}}^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+\sqrt{T}{\mathbb{E}}_{A^{\prime}}\left[\lVert X^{A^{\prime}}\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})^{p}}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}\sqrt{T}\lVert\tilde{\beta}_{n}-\tilde{\beta}\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})^{p}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\sqrt{T}\sqrt{\lVert\tilde{\beta}_{n}\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})^{p}}+\lVert\tilde{\beta}\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})^{p}}},

which converges to zero (note that
\{\lVert\tilde{\beta}_{n}\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})^{p}}\}_{n\in{\mathbb{N}}}
is bounded since
\lVert\tilde{\beta}_{n}-\tilde{\beta}\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})^{p}}\to 0
), proving the claim.
∎

Combining Claim
A.3
with (
A.60
) implies that

\displaystyle\limsup_{n\to\infty}R_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}(\beta_{n})\leq R_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}(\beta),

(A.61)

for any
\beta\in L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})^{p}
. Given that
\{\lambda(n)\}_{n\in{\mathbb{N}}}
contains a subsequence,
\{\lambda(n_{k})\}_{k\in{\mathbb{N}}}
that converges in
\mathit{l}^{2}
with a corresponding limit
\lambda\in\mathit{l}^{2}
then also
\beta_{n_{k}}\xrightarrow{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})^{p}}\beta^{\prime}
for some
\beta^{\prime}\in L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})^{p}
. By (
A.61
), it follows that
R_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}(\beta^{\prime})\leq R_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}(\beta)
for all
\beta\in L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})^{p}
, i.e.
\beta^{\prime}\in S\not=\emptyset
. This proves the first claim of the theorem. For the second claim suppose
dist(\beta_{n},S)\not\to 0
then there is a subsequence
\{\beta_{n_{k}}\}_{k}
such that
dist(\beta_{n_{k}},S)>\delta
for some
\delta>0
and every
k\in{\mathbb{N}}
. By assumption we may then extract a further subsequence
\{\beta_{n_{k_{l}}}\}_{l\in{\mathbb{N}}}
such that
\beta_{n_{k_{l}}}\xrightarrow{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})^{p}}\beta^{\prime\prime}
for some
\beta^{\prime\prime}\in L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})^{p}
. But since
\beta^{\prime\prime}\not\in S
,
R_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}(\beta^{\prime\prime})>R_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}(\beta)
for every
\beta\in S
, but due to (
A.61
) and Claim
A.3
,

R_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}(\beta^{\prime\prime})=\lim_{l\to\infty}R_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}\left(\beta_{n_{k_{l}}}\right)\leq R_{\sqrt{\gamma}A}(\beta),

for any
\beta\in L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})^{p}
. This contradiction proves the second claim.
∎

A.8
Proof of Theorem
5.1

We provide the proof for the case
p=1
. The case
p>1
is analogous, just more notationally cumbersome.

Proof.

Step 1: Use the whole sample curves.

We first fix
E\in{\mathbb{N}}
and let

C_{l}^{A,m}=\langle X^{A,m},\psi_{l}\rangle_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}
,
C_{l}^{O,m}=\langle X^{O,m},\psi_{l}\rangle_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}
,

D_{k}^{A,m}=\langle Y^{A,m},\phi_{k}\rangle_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}
,
D_{k}^{O,m}=\langle Y^{O,m},\phi_{k}\rangle_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}
,

\beta^{(m),E}=\sum_{k=1}^{E}\sum_{l=1}^{E}\phi_{k}\otimes\psi_{l}\frac{\gamma C_{l}^{A,m}D_{k}^{A,m}+(1-\gamma)C_{l}^{O,m}D_{k}^{O,m}}{{\mathbb{E}}\left[\gamma(\chi_{l}^{A})^{2}+(1-\gamma)(\chi_{l}^{O})^{2}\right]}

\beta_{E}=\sum_{k=1}^{E}\sum_{l=1}^{E}\phi_{k}\otimes\psi_{l}\frac{{\mathbb{E}}\left[\gamma\chi_{l}^{A}Z_{k}^{A}+(1-\gamma)\chi_{l}^{O}Z_{k}^{O}\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}\left[\gamma(\chi_{l}^{A})^{2}+(1-\gamma)(\chi_{l}^{O})^{2}\right]}

\hat{\beta}_{n,E,(1)}=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}\beta^{(m),E}

Q_{l}=\gamma(\chi_{l}^{A})^{2}+(1-\gamma)(\chi_{l}^{O})^{2}

W_{l}^{m}=\gamma(C_{l}^{\prime A,m})^{2}+(1-\gamma)(C_{l}^{\prime O,m})^{2}
and

U_{l,k}^{m}=\gamma C_{l}^{A,m}D_{k}^{A,m}+(1-\gamma)C_{l}^{O,m}D_{k}^{O,m}

Note that by orthogonality of the basis functions and the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\beta^{(m),E}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]^{2}}\left(\sum_{l=1}^{E}\sum_{k=1}^{E}\frac{U_{l,k}^{m}}{{\mathbb{E}}\left[Q_{l}\right]}\phi_{k}(t)\psi_{l}(\tau)\right)^{2}dtd\tau\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(\sum_{l=1}^{E}\sum_{k=1}^{E}\sum_{q=1}^{E}\sum_{r=1}^{E}\frac{U_{l,k}^{m}U_{q,r}^{m}\delta_{l,q}\delta_{k,r}}{{\mathbb{E}}\left[Q_{l}\right]{\mathbb{E}}\left[Q_{q}\right]}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(\sum_{l=1}^{E}\sum_{k=1}^{E}\frac{\left(U_{l,k}^{m}\right)^{2}}{{\mathbb{E}}\left[Q_{l}\right]^{2}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{l=1}^{E}\sum_{k=1}^{E}\frac{{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|\gamma\chi_{l}^{A}Z_{k}^{A}+\chi_{l}^{O}Z_{k}^{O}\right|\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}\left[Q_{l}\right]}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{l=1}^{E}\sum_{k=1}^{E}\frac{\gamma{\mathbb{E}}\left[(\chi_{l}^{A})^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[(Z_{k}^{A})^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}+{\mathbb{E}}\left[(\chi_{l}^{O})^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[(Z_{k}^{O})^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}}{{\mathbb{E}}\left[Q_{l}\right]}<\infty.

(A.62)

By the Banach space version of the law of large numbers (see for instance
Bosq 2000
, Theorem 2.4) it follows that for every
E\in{\mathbb{N}}
,
\|\hat{\beta}_{n,E,(1)}-\beta_{E}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}\xrightarrow{a.s.}0
. Let
f_{n}(\omega)=\|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}\beta^{(m),E}-\beta_{E}\|
and
g_{n}(\omega)=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}\|\beta^{(m),E}\|+\|\beta_{E}\|
. Then
0\leq f_{n}\leq g_{n}
,
f_{n}\xrightarrow{a.s.}0
, (by the scalar law of large numbers)
g_{n}\xrightarrow{a.s.}G={\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\beta^{(1),E}\|\right]+\|\beta_{E}\|
and
{\mathbb{E}}\left[g_{n}\right]={\mathbb{E}}\left[G\right]
. It now follows from Pratt’s lemma that
{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\hat{\beta}_{n,E,(1)}-\beta_{E}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}\ \right]\to 0
, for every
E\in{\mathbb{N}}
. If we now let
\{e(n)\}_{n\in{\mathbb{N}}}
be a sequence in
{\mathbb{N}}
tending to infinity, such that
e(n)\leq E_{1}(n)
where
E_{1}(1)=1
, and for
n>1
,

\displaystyle E_{1}(n)=(E_{1}(n-1)+1)1_{{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\hat{\beta}_{n,E_{1}(n-1)+1,(1)}-\beta_{E_{1}(n-1)+1}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}\right]<\alpha_{E_{1}(n-1)+1}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle E_{1}(n-1)1_{{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\hat{\beta}_{n,E_{1}(n-1)+1,(1)}-\beta_{E_{1}(n-1)+1}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}\right]\geq\alpha_{E_{1}(n-1)+1}},

for some non-increasing sequence of positive numbers
\{\alpha_{n}\}_{n\in{\mathbb{N}}}
that tend to zero. With this construction it follows that if we let
\hat{B}_{n,(1)}=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}B^{(m),e(n)}
and assume
e(n)\leq E_{1}(n)
then

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\hat{\beta}_{n,(1)}-\beta\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\hat{\beta}_{n,(1)}-\beta_{e(n)}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}+\sqrt{2}\|\beta-\beta_{e(n)}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{2}\alpha_{e(n)}+\sqrt{2\sum_{k=e(n)+1}^{\infty}\sum_{l=e(n)+1}^{\infty}\frac{{\mathbb{E}}\left[U_{l,k}^{m}\right]^{2}}{{\mathbb{E}}\left[Q_{l}\right]^{2}}},

(A.63)

which converges to zero. By the Markov inequality
\|\hat{\beta}_{n,(1)}-\beta\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}\xrightarrow{{\mathbb{P}}}0
.

Step 2: Replace the population denominators.

Let

\beta^{(m),2}=\sum_{k=1}^{e(n)}\sum_{l=1}^{e(n)}\frac{U_{l,k}^{m}}{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}W_{l}^{m}}\phi_{k}\otimes\phi_{l},

\hat{\beta}_{n,(2)}=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}\beta^{(m),2}
and

A_{n,1}=\left\{\frac{{\mathbb{E}}\left[Q_{l}\right]^{2}}{2}\leq\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}W_{l}^{m}\right)^{2}\leq 2{\mathbb{E}}\left[Q_{l}\right]^{2},l=1,..,e(n)\right\}.

Let
E_{2}(n)
be a sequence tending to infinity such that if
e(n)\leq E_{1}(n)\vee E_{2}(n)
,
\lim_{n\to\infty}{\mathbb{P}}\left(A_{n,1}\right)=1
.

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\hat{\beta}_{n,(2)}-\hat{\beta}_{n,(1)}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}1_{A_{n,1}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}\|B^{(m),1}-\beta^{(m),2}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}1_{A_{n,1}}\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(\sum_{l=1}^{e(n)}\sum_{k=1}^{e(n)}(U_{l,k}^{m})^{2}\left|\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}\left[Q_{l}\right]^{2}}-\frac{1}{\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}W_{l}^{m}\right)^{2}}\right|\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}1_{A_{n,1}}\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(\sum_{l=1}^{e(n)}\sum_{k=1}^{e(n)}(U_{l,k}m)^{2}\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}\left[Q_{l}\right]^{2}\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}W_{l}^{m}\right)^{2}}\left|{\mathbb{E}}\left[Q_{l}\right]^{2}-\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}W_{l}^{m}\right)^{2}\right|\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}1_{A_{n,1}}\right]

(A.64)

due to independence we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(\sum_{l=1}^{e(n)}\sum_{k=1}^{e(n)}(U_{l,k}^{m})^{2}\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}\left[Q_{l}\right]^{2}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}W_{l}^{m}}\left|{\mathbb{E}}\left[Q_{l}\right]^{2}-\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}W_{l}^{m}\right)^{2}\right|\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}1_{A_{n,1}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{l=1}^{e(n)}\sum_{k=1}^{e(n)}\frac{{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|U_{l,k}^{m}\right|\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}\left[Q_{l}\right]}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|\frac{{\mathbb{E}}\left[Q_{l}\right]^{2}-\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}W_{l}^{m}\right)^{2}}{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}W_{l}^{m}}\right|^{\frac{1}{2}}1_{A_{n,1}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{l=1}^{e(n)}\sum_{k=1}^{e(n)}\frac{{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|U_{l,k}^{m}\right|\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}\left[Q_{l}\right]}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|\frac{{\mathbb{E}}\left[Q_{l}\right]^{2}-\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}W_{l}^{m}\right)^{2}}{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}W_{l}^{m}}\right|1_{A_{n,1}}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}},

Noting that

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|\frac{{\mathbb{E}}\left[Q_{l}\right]^{2}-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}W_{l}^{m}}{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}W_{l}^{m}}\right|1_{A_{n,1}}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{2}\frac{{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|{\mathbb{E}}\left[Q_{l}\right]^{2}-\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}W_{l}^{m}\right)^{2}\right|1_{A_{n,1}}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}}{{\mathbb{E}}\left[Q_{l}\right]}:=a_{l}(n),

where, by dominated convergence,
\lim_{n\to\infty}a_{l}(n)=0
for every
l
.
This implies

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\hat{\beta}_{n,(2)}-\hat{\beta}_{n,(1)}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}1_{A_{n,1}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{l=1}^{e(n)}\sum_{k=1}^{e(n)}\frac{{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|\gamma\chi^{A}_{l}Z^{A}_{k}+(1-\gamma)\chi^{O}_{l}Z^{O}_{k}\right|\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}\left[Q_{l}\right]}\sqrt{2}a_{l}(n).

We now let
E_{3}(1)=1
, and for
n>1
,

\displaystyle E_{3}(n)

\displaystyle=(E_{3}(n-1)+1)1_{\sum_{l=1}^{E_{3}(n-1)+1}\sum_{k=1}^{E_{3}(n-1)+1}\frac{{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|\gamma\chi^{A}_{l}Z^{A}_{k}+(1-\gamma)\chi^{O}_{l}Z^{O}_{k}\right|\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}\left[Q_{l}\right]}\sqrt{2}a_{l}(n)<\alpha_{n}}

\displaystyle+E_{3}(n-1)1_{\sum_{l=1}^{E_{3}(n-1)+1}\sum_{k=1}^{E_{3}(n-1)+1}\frac{{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|\gamma\chi^{A}_{l}Z^{A}_{k}+(1-\gamma)\chi^{O}_{l}Z^{O}_{k}\right|\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}\left[Q_{l}\right]}\sqrt{2}a_{l}(n)\geq\alpha_{n}}.

It follows from Markov’s inequality that if
e(n)\leq E_{1}(n)\vee E_{2}(n)\vee E_{3}(n)

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}\left(\|\hat{\beta}_{n,(2)}-\hat{\beta}_{n,(1)}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}\geq\epsilon\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\epsilon}\sum_{l=1}^{e(n)}\sum_{k=1}^{e(n)}\frac{{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|\gamma\chi^{A}_{l}Z^{A}_{k}+(1-\gamma)\chi^{O}_{l}Z^{O}_{k}\right|\right]}{{\mathbb{E}}\left[Q_{l}\right]}\sqrt{2}a_{l}(n)+{\mathbb{P}}\left(A_{n,1}^{c}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\alpha_{n}+{\mathbb{P}}\left(A_{n,1}^{c}\right),

which converges to zero for every
\epsilon>0
.

Step 3: Discretize the sample curves for the numerators.

Recall (from Section
5
) the definitions of
C_{l}^{A,m,n}
and
D_{k}^{A,m,d,n}
. Let

U_{l,k}^{m,n}=\gamma C_{l}^{A,m,n}D_{k}^{A,m,n}+(1-\gamma)C_{l}^{O,m,n}D_{k}^{O,m,n}
,

\beta^{(m),3}=\sum_{k=1}^{e(n)}\sum_{l=1}^{e(n)}\frac{U_{l,k}^{m,n}}{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}W_{l}^{m}}\phi_{k}\otimes\psi_{l}
and

\hat{\beta}_{n,(3)}=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}\beta^{(m),3}
.

On the set
A_{n,1}

\displaystyle\|\hat{\beta}_{n,(3)}-\hat{\beta}_{n,(2)}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}\|\beta^{(m),3}-\beta^{(m),2}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}\left(\sum_{l=1}^{e(n)}\sum_{k=1}^{e(n)}\sum_{q=1}^{e(n)}\sum_{r=1}^{e(n)}\frac{\left(U_{l,k}^{m}-U_{l,k}^{m,n}\right)\left(U_{r,q}-U_{r,q}^{n}\right)}{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}W_{l}^{m}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}W_{r}^{m}}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\phi_{k}(t)\phi_{r}(t)dt\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\psi_{l}(\tau)\psi_{q}(\tau)d\tau\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{e(n)}\sum_{l=1}^{e(n)}\frac{\left(U_{l,k}^{m}-U_{l,k}^{m,n}\right)^{2}}{\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}W_{l}^{m}\right)^{2}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{e(n)}\sum_{l=1}^{e(n)}\frac{1}{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}W_{l}^{m}}\left(\gamma C_{l}^{A,m,n}(D_{k}^{A,m,n}-D_{k}^{A,m})+\gamma D_{k}^{A,m}(C_{l}^{A,m,n}-C_{l}^{A,m})\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.+(1-\gamma)C_{l}^{O,m,n}(D_{k}^{O,m,n}-D_{k}^{O,m})+(1-\gamma)D_{k}^{O,m}(C_{l}^{O,m,n}-C_{l}^{O,m})\right)^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{e(n)}\sum_{l=1}^{e(n)}\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}\left[Q_{l}\right]}\left(\left(8\left(\gamma C_{l}^{A,m,n}(D_{k}^{A,m,n}-D_{k}^{A,m})\right)^{2}\right.\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.\left.+8\left(\gamma D_{k}^{A,m}(C_{l}^{A,m,n}-C_{l}^{A,m})\right)^{2}+8\left((1-\gamma)C_{l}^{O,m,n}(D_{k}^{O,m,n}-D_{k}^{O,m})\right)^{2}\right.\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.\left.+8\left((1-\gamma)D_{k}^{O,m}(C_{l}^{O,m,n}-C_{l}^{O,m})\right)^{2}\right)\right)\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Note that by Cauchy-Schwarz inequality

\displaystyle(C_{l}^{A,m,n}-C_{l}^{A,m})^{2}

\displaystyle=\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(P_{\Pi_{n}}(X^{A,m},t)-X^{A}_{t}\right)\psi_{l}(t)dt\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left|P_{\Pi_{n}}(X^{A,m},t)-X^{A}_{t}\right|\left|\psi_{l}(t)\right|dt\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left|P_{\Pi_{n}}(X^{A,m},t)-X^{A}_{t}\right|^{2}dt\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left|\psi_{l}(t)\right|^{2}dt\leq Td_{n}^{2}

and similarly

\displaystyle(D_{k}^{A,m,n}-D_{k}^{A,m})^{2}

\displaystyle=\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left(P_{\Pi_{n}}(Y^{A,m},s)-Y^{A}_{s}\right)\psi_{l}(s)ds\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left|P_{\Pi_{n}}(Y^{A,m},s)-Y^{A}_{s}\right|^{2}ds\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left|\psi_{l}(s)\right|^{2}ds\leq Td_{n}^{2}.

Furthermore

\displaystyle(C_{l}^{A,m,n})^{2}

\displaystyle=\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}P_{\Pi_{n}}(X^{A,m},t)\psi_{l}(t)dt\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left|P_{\Pi_{n}}(X^{A,m},t)\right|\left|\psi_{l}(t)\right|dt\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left|P_{\Pi_{n}}(X^{A,m},t)\right|^{2}dt

\displaystyle\leq 2\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left|P_{\Pi_{n}}(X^{A,m},t)-X_{t}^{A,m}\right|^{2}dt+2\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left|X_{t}^{A,m}\right|^{2}dt\leq 2Td_{n}^{2}+2\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}\left|X_{t}^{A,m}\right|^{2}dt.

Letting

M_{n}=\max_{1\leq m\leq e(n)}\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(Y_{s}^{A,m})^{2}ds+\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(Y_{s}^{O,m})^{2}ds+\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(X_{s}^{A,m})^{2}ds+\int_{[T_{1},T_{2}]}(X_{s}^{O,m})^{2}ds,

we get on the set
A_{n,1}
,

\displaystyle\|\hat{\beta}_{n,(3)}-\hat{\beta}_{n,(2)}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}\left(2\frac{e(n)^{2}(8(1-\gamma)^{2}+8\gamma^{2})Td_{n}^{2}(2Td_{n}^{2}+2M_{n})}{\min_{1\leq l\leq e(n)}{\mathbb{E}}\left[Q_{l}\right]}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq 2^{\frac{5}{2}}\sqrt{2+3\gamma^{2}}Te(n)d_{n}\frac{\left(\sqrt{M_{n}}+\sqrt{T}d_{n}\right)}{\sqrt{\min_{1\leq l\leq e(n)}{\mathbb{E}}\left[Q_{l}\right]}}.

Therefore

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}\left(\right\{\|\hat{\beta}_{n,(3)}-\hat{\beta}_{n,(2)}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}\geq\epsilon\left\}\cap A_{n,1}\right)\leq{\mathbb{P}}\left(M_{n}\geq\left(\frac{\epsilon\sqrt{\min_{1\leq l\leq e(n)}{\mathbb{E}}\left[Q_{l}\right]}}{2^{\frac{5}{2}}\sqrt{2+3\gamma^{2}}Te(n)d_{n}}-\sqrt{T}d_{n}\right)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 1-F_{\|X^{A}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]}^{2}+\|X^{O}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]}^{2}+\|Y^{A}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]}^{2}+\|Y^{O}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]}^{2}}\left(\left(\frac{\epsilon\sqrt{\min_{1\leq l\leq e(n)}{\mathbb{E}}\left[Q_{l}\right]}}{2^{\frac{5}{2}}\sqrt{2+3\gamma^{2}}Te(n)d_{n}}-\sqrt{T}d_{n}\right)^{2}\right)^{e(n)}

\displaystyle\leq 1-\left(1-{\mathbb{P}}\left(\|X^{A}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]}^{2}+\|X^{O}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]}^{2}+\|Y^{A}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]}^{2}+\|Y^{O}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]}^{2}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.\geq\left(\frac{\epsilon\sqrt{\min_{1\leq l\leq e(n)}{\mathbb{E}}\left[Q_{l}\right]}}{2^{\frac{5}{2}}\sqrt{2+3\gamma^{2}}Te(n)d_{n}}-\sqrt{T}d_{n}\right)^{2}\right)\right)^{e(n)}

\displaystyle\leq 1-\left(1-\frac{{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|X^{A}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]}^{2}+\|X^{O}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]}^{2}+\|Y^{A}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]}^{2}+\|Y^{O}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]}^{2}\right]}{\left(\frac{\epsilon\sqrt{\min_{1\leq l\leq e(n)}{\mathbb{E}}\left[Q_{l}\right]}}{2^{\frac{5}{2}}\sqrt{2+3\gamma^{2}}Te(n)d_{n}}-\sqrt{T}d_{n}\right)^{2}}\right)^{e(n)}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{e(n){\mathbb{E}}\left[\|X^{A}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]}^{2}+\|X^{O}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]}^{2}+\|Y^{A}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]}^{2}+\|Y^{O}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]}^{2}\right]}{\left(\frac{\epsilon\sqrt{\min_{1\leq l\leq e(n)}{\mathbb{E}}\left[Q_{l}\right]}}{2^{\frac{5}{2}}\sqrt{2+3\gamma^{2}}Te(n)d_{n}}-\sqrt{T}d_{n}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{e(n)^{3}d_{n}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|X^{A}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]}^{2}+\|X^{O}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]}^{2}+\|Y^{A}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]}^{2}+\|Y^{O}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]}^{2}\right]}{\frac{\epsilon^{2}\min_{1\leq l\leq e(n)}{\mathbb{E}}\left[Q_{l}\right]}{2^{5}T^{2}(2+3\gamma^{2})}-e(n)^{2}d_{n}^{2}},

so if
e(n)\leq d_{n}^{\eta}
, where
\eta\in\left(-\frac{2}{3},0\right)
then the above expression will converge to zero. Set
E_{4}(n)=d_{n}^{\eta}
with
\eta
as prescribed.

Step 4: Discretize the sample curves for the denominators.

Let

W_{l}^{m,n}=\gamma(C_{l}^{\prime A,m,n})^{2}+(1-\gamma)(C_{l}^{\prime O,m,n})^{2}

\beta^{(m),4}=\sum_{k=1}^{e(n)}\sum_{l=1}^{e(n)}\frac{U_{l,k}^{m,n}}{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}W_{l}^{m,n}}\phi_{k}\otimes\psi_{l}
and

\hat{\beta}_{n,(4)}=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}\beta^{(m),4}=\hat{\beta}_{n}
.

Noting that

\left|C_{l}^{\prime A,m,n}-C_{l}^{\prime A,m}\right|\leq d_{n}
implies

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}\left(C_{l}^{\prime A,m,n}\right)^{2}-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}\left(C_{l}^{\prime A,m}\right)^{2}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}\left|C_{l}^{\prime A,m,n}-C_{l}^{\prime A,m}\right|\left|C_{l}^{\prime A,m,n}+C_{l}^{\prime A,m}\right|

\displaystyle\leq d_{n}(1+d_{n})\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}\left|C_{l}^{\prime A,m}\right|\leq d_{n}(1+d_{n})\frac{1}{n}\left(\sum_{m=1}^{n}\left(C_{l}^{\prime A,m}\right)^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

with an analogous inequality being valid in the observational setting. This leads to

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{l=m}^{n}\left(\gamma\left(C_{l}^{\prime A,m,n}\right)^{2}+(1-\gamma)\left(C_{l}^{\prime O,m,n}\right)^{2}\right)-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}\left(\gamma\left(C_{l}^{\prime A,m}\right)^{2}+(1-\gamma)\left(C_{l}^{\prime O,m}\right)^{2}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\gamma d_{n}(1+d_{n})\frac{1}{n}\left(\sum_{m=1}^{n}\left(C_{l}^{\prime A,m}\right)^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+(1-\gamma)d_{n}(1+d_{n})\frac{1}{n}\left(\sum_{m=1}^{n}\left(C_{l}^{\prime O,m}\right)^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Moreover, on
A_{n,1}

\displaystyle\left(C_{l}^{\prime A,m,n}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\left(\left|C_{l}^{\prime A,m,n}\right|-d_{n}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\left(C_{l}^{\prime A,m,n}\right)^{2}+d_{n}^{2}-4d_{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[(\chi_{l}^{A})^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}\geq\frac{1}{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[(\chi_{l}^{A})^{2}\right]+d_{n}^{2}-4d_{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[(\chi_{l}^{A})^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}

and

\displaystyle\left(C_{l}^{\prime O,m,n}\right)^{2}\geq\frac{1}{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[(\chi_{l}^{O})^{2}\right]+d_{n}^{2}-4d_{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[(\chi_{l}^{O})^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Therefore, letting

R_{l,n}={\mathbb{E}}\left[Q_{l}\right]\left(\gamma\left(\frac{1}{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[(\chi_{l}^{A})^{2}\right]+d_{n}^{2}-4d_{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[(\chi_{l}^{A})^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)+(1-\gamma)\left(\frac{1}{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[(\chi_{l}^{O})^{2}\right]+d_{n}^{2}-4d_{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[(\chi_{l}^{O})^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\right)

we have
\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}W_{l}^{m}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}W_{l}^{m,n}\geq R_{l,n}
. On
A_{n,1}
,

\displaystyle\|\hat{\beta}_{n}-\hat{\beta}_{n,(3)}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{e(n)}\sum_{l=1}^{e(n)}(U_{l,k}^{m,n})^{2}\left(\frac{1}{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}W_{l}^{m}}-\frac{1}{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}W_{l}^{m,n}}\right)^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{e(n)}\sum_{l=1}^{e(n)}\frac{(U_{l,k}^{m,n})^{2}}{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}W_{l}^{m}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}W_{l}^{m,n}}\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}W_{l}^{m}-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}W_{l}^{m,n}\right)^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{e(n)}\sum_{l=1}^{e(n)}\frac{2(U_{l,k}^{m,n})^{2}}{R_{l,n}}d_{n}^{2}(1+d_{n})^{2}\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{m=1}^{n}W_{l}^{m}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

This leads to,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\hat{\beta}_{n}-\hat{\beta}_{n,(3)}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}1_{A_{n,1}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k=1}^{e(n)}\sum_{l=1}^{e(n)}\frac{1}{R_{l,n}}2{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|U_{l,k}^{m,n}\right|\right]{\mathbb{E}}\left[d_{n}(1+d_{n})\frac{1}{n}\left(\sum_{m=1}^{n}W_{l}^{m}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k=1}^{e(n)}\sum_{l=1}^{e(n)}\frac{1}{R_{l,n}}\left(2{\mathbb{E}}\left[\gamma\left(\left|C_{l}^{A,m}\right|+Td_{n}\right)\left(\left|D_{k}^{A,m}\right|+Td_{n}\right)\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.+(1-\gamma)\left(\left|C_{l}^{O,m}\right|+Td_{n}\right)\left(\left|D_{k}^{O,m}\right|+Td_{n}\right)\right]\right)d_{n}(1+d_{n}){\mathbb{E}}\left[Q_{l}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq d_{n}(1+d_{n})\sum_{k=1}^{e(n)}\sum_{l=1}^{e(n)}\frac{1}{R_{l,n}}\left(2\left(T^{2}d_{n}^{2}+T\gamma{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|\chi_{l}^{A}\right|+\left|Z_{k}^{A}\right|\right]\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.+\gamma{\mathbb{E}}\left[(\chi_{l}^{A})^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[(Z_{k}^{A})^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}+T(1-\gamma){\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|\chi_{l}^{O}\right|+\left|Z_{k}^{O}\right|\right]\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.+(1-\gamma){\mathbb{E}}\left[(\chi_{l}^{O})^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[(D_{k}^{O})^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[Q_{l}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}

letting
g_{4}
be a strictly increasing function such that

\displaystyle\lim_{E\to\infty}\frac{1}{g_{4}(E)}\sum_{k=1}^{E}\sum_{l=1}^{E}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{R_{l,n}}\left(2\left(T^{2}d_{n}^{2}+T\gamma{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|\chi_{l}^{A}\right|+\left|Z_{k}^{A}\right|\right]+\gamma{\mathbb{E}}\left[(\chi_{l}^{A})^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[(Z_{k}^{A})^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}+T(1-\gamma){\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|\chi_{l}^{O}\right|+\left|Z_{k}^{O}\right|\right]\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.+(1-\gamma){\mathbb{E}}\left[(\chi_{l}^{O})^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[(D_{k}^{O})^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[Q_{l}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}=0,

letting

e(n)\leq E_{5}(n)=g_{4}^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{d_{n}(1+d_{n})}\right)

is sufficient to ensure that
\|\hat{B}_{n,(4)}-\hat{B}_{n,(3)}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}\xrightarrow{{\mathbb{P}}}0
.

Step 5: Replace the population eigenfunctions with the truncated estimators.

Let
B^{(m),5}=\sum_{k=1}^{e(n)}\sum_{l=1}^{e(n)}\frac{U_{l,k}^{m,n}}{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}W_{l}^{m,n}}\phi_{k}\otimes T_{M}\left(\hat{\psi}_{l,n}\right)
and
\hat{B}_{n,(5)}=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}B^{(m),5}(t,\tau)
. We then have the following bound

\displaystyle\|B^{(m),5}-B^{(m),4}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k=1}^{e(n)}\sum_{l=1}^{e(n)}\frac{\left|U_{l,k}^{m,n}\right|}{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}W_{l}^{m,n}}\|\phi_{k}\otimes\left(T_{M}\left(\hat{\psi}_{l,n}\right)-\psi_{l}\right)\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}

\displaystyle\leq\gamma\sum_{k=1}^{e(n)}\left|D_{k}^{A,m,n}\right|\|\phi_{k}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}\sum_{l=1}^{e(n)}\frac{\left|C_{l}^{A,m,n}\right|}{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}W_{l}^{m,n}}\|T_{M}\left(\hat{\psi}_{l,n}\right)-\psi_{l}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}

\displaystyle+|1-\gamma|\sum_{k=1}^{e(n)}\left|D_{k}^{O,m,n}\right|\|\phi_{k}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}\sum_{l=1}^{e(n)}\frac{\left|C_{l}^{O,m,n}\right|}{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}W_{l}^{m,n}}\|T_{M}\left(\hat{\psi}_{l,n}\right)-\psi_{l}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}

\displaystyle=(1+\gamma)\sum_{k=1}^{e(n)}\left(\left|D_{k}^{A,m,n}\right|+\left|D_{k}^{O,m,n}\right|\right)\sum_{l=1}^{e(n)}\frac{\left|C_{l}^{A,m,n}\right|+\left|C_{l}^{O,m,n}\right|}{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}W_{l}^{m,n}}\|T_{M}\left(\hat{\psi}_{l,n}\right)-\psi_{l}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}.

(A.65)

By the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\hat{B}_{n,(5)}-\hat{B}_{n,(4)}\|1_{A_{n,1}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|B^{(m),5}-B^{(m),4}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}1_{A_{n,1}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[(1+\gamma)\sum_{k=1}^{e(n)}\left(\left|D_{k}^{A,m,n}\right|+\left|D_{k}^{O,m,n}\right|\right)\sum_{l=1}^{e(n)}\frac{\left|C_{l}^{A,m,n}\right|+\left|C_{l}^{O,m,n}\right|}{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}W_{l}^{m,n}}\|T_{M}\left(\hat{\psi}_{l,n}\right)-\psi_{l}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}1_{A_{n,1}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq(1+\gamma)\sum_{l=1}^{e(n)}\sum_{k=1}^{e(n)}{\mathbb{E}}\left[2\left|D_{k}^{A,m,n}\right|^{2}+2\left|D_{k}^{O,m,n}\right|^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{2\left|C_{l}^{A,m,n}\right|^{2}+2\left|C_{l}^{O,m,n}\right|^{2}}{\frac{R_{l,n}}{{\mathbb{E}}\left[Q_{l}\right]}}\|T_{M}\left(\hat{\psi}_{l,n}\right)-\psi_{l}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}^{2}1_{A_{n,1}}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}.

(A.66)

Letting
*
be either
A
or
O
, note that

\displaystyle\left|C_{l}^{*,m,n}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\int\left|P_{\Pi_{n}}(X^{*,m},t)\psi_{l}(t)\right|dt

\displaystyle\leq\|P_{\Pi_{n}}(X^{*,m},.)\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}\|\psi_{l}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}=\|P_{\Pi_{n}}(X^{*,m},.)\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}

It is straight-forward to show that

\|P_{\Pi_{n}}(X^{*,m},.)-X^{*,m}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}^{2}\leq Td_{n}^{2}

and therefore

\displaystyle\|P_{\Pi_{n}}(X^{*,m},.)\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}

\displaystyle\leq\|P_{\Pi_{n}}(X^{*,m},.)-X\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}+\|X^{*,m}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{T}d_{n}+\|X^{*,m}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}.

Similarly
