\displaystyle\|P_{\Pi_{n}}(Y^{*,m},.)\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}\leq\sqrt{T}d_{n}+\|Y^{*,m}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}.

This leads to

\displaystyle\left|C_{l}^{*,m,n}\right|^{2}

\displaystyle=\|P_{\Pi_{n}}(X^{*,m},.)\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left(Td_{n}^{2}+\|X^{*,m}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}^{2}\right)

and similarly

\displaystyle\left|D_{k}^{*,m,n}\right|^{2}\leq 2\left(Td_{n}^{2}+\|Y^{*,m}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}^{2}\right).

Utilizing the independence of the
\{\psi_{l,n}\}_{l}
from
\mathcal{F}_{n}
, we plug these findings back into (
A.8
) and get

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\hat{B}_{n,(5)}-\hat{B}_{n,(4)}\|1_{A_{n,1}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq(1+\gamma)\sum_{l=1}^{e(n)}\sum_{k=1}^{e(n)}\sqrt{2}\left(2Td_{n}^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|Y^{A,m}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}^{2}+\|Y^{O,m}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}^{2}\right]\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\times{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{2\left(Td_{n}^{2}+\|X^{A,m}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}^{2}+\|X^{O,m}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}^{2}\right)\|T_{M}\left(\hat{\psi}_{l,n}\right)-\psi_{l}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}^{2}}{\frac{R_{l,n}}{{\mathbb{E}}\left[Q_{l}\right]}}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq(1+\gamma)e(n)\sum_{l=1}^{e(n)}\sqrt{2}\left(2Td_{n}^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|Y^{A}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}^{2}+\|Y^{O}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}^{2}\right]\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\sqrt{\frac{{\mathbb{E}}\left[Q_{l}\right]}{R_{l,n}}}

\displaystyle\times{\mathbb{E}}\left[2\left(Td_{n}^{2}+\|X^{A}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}^{2}+\|X^{O}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}^{2}\right)\sum_{l=1}^{e(n)}\|T_{M}\left(\hat{\psi}_{l,n}\right)-\psi_{l}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle=(1+\gamma)2\left(Td_{n}^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|Y^{A}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}^{2}+\|Y^{O}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}^{2}\right]\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[Td_{n}^{2}+\|X^{A}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}^{2}+\|X^{O}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\times\sum_{l=1}^{e(n)}\sqrt{\frac{{\mathbb{E}}\left[Q_{l}\right]}{R_{l,n}}}e(n){\mathbb{E}}\left[\sum_{l=1}^{e(n)}\|T_{M}\left(\hat{\psi}_{l,n}\right)-\psi_{l}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq 2(1+\gamma)\left(Td_{n}^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|Y^{A}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}^{2}+\|Y^{O}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}^{2}\right]\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[Td_{n}^{2}+\|X^{A}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}^{2}+\|X^{O}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\times\sum_{l=1}^{e(n)}\sqrt{\frac{{\mathbb{E}}\left[Q_{l}\right]}{R_{l,n}}}e(n)\sum_{l=1}^{e(n)}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|T_{M}\left(\hat{\psi}_{l,n}\right)-\psi_{l}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}.

(A.67)

Since
e(n)\sum_{l=1}^{e(n)}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|T_{M}\left(\hat{\psi}_{l,n}\right)-\psi_{l}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}
converges to zero by assumption we may define
E_{6}(1)=1
and for
n>1

\displaystyle E_{6}(n)

\displaystyle=(E_{6}(n-1)+1)1_{(E_{6}(n-1)+1)\sum_{l=1}^{E_{6}(n-1)+1}\sqrt{\frac{{\mathbb{E}}\left[Q_{l}\right]}{R_{l,n}}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|T_{M}\left(\hat{\psi}_{l,n}\right)-\psi_{l}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}<\alpha_{n}}

\displaystyle+E_{6}(n-1)1_{(E_{6}(n-1)+1)\sum_{l=1}^{E_{6}(n-1)+1}\sqrt{\frac{{\mathbb{E}}\left[Q_{l}\right]}{R_{l,n}}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|T_{M}\left(\hat{\psi}_{l,n}\right)-\psi_{l}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}\geq\alpha_{n}}.

It follows by Markov’s inequality that
\|\hat{B}_{n,(5)}-\hat{B}_{n,(4)}\|\xrightarrow{{\mathbb{P}}}0
.

Step 6: replace the numerators using the estimated eigenfunctions.

Let

\tilde{C}_{l}^{A,m,n}=\int P_{\Pi_{n}}(X^{A,m},t)\hat{\psi}_{l}(t)dt,

\tilde{C}_{l}^{O,m,n}=\int P_{\Pi_{n}}(X^{O,m},t)\hat{\psi}_{l}(t)dt,

B^{(m),6}=\sum_{k=1}^{e(n)}\sum_{l=1}^{e(n)}\frac{\gamma\tilde{C}_{l}^{A,m,n}D_{k}^{A,m,n}+(1-\gamma)\tilde{C}_{l}^{O,m,n}D_{k}^{O,m,n}}{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}W_{l}^{m,n}}\phi_{k}\otimes T_{M}\left(\hat{\psi}_{l,n}\right)

and
\hat{B}_{n,(6)}=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}B^{(m),6}(t,\tau)
. We then have the following bound

\displaystyle\|B^{(m),6}-B^{(m),5}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k=1}^{e(n)}\sum_{l=1}^{e(n)}(1+\gamma)\frac{\left|C_{l}^{A,m,n}-\tilde{C}_{l}^{A,m,n}\right|+\left|C_{l}^{O,m,n}-\tilde{C}_{l}^{O,m,n}\right|}{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{p=1}^{n}W_{l}^{m,n}}\left(\left|D_{k}^{A,m,n}\right|+\left|D_{k}^{O,m,n}\right|\right)\|\phi_{k}\otimes T_{M}\left(\hat{\psi}_{l,n}\right)\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}

\displaystyle\leq(1+\gamma)\sum_{k=1}^{e(n)}\left(\left|D_{k}^{A,m,n}\right|+\left|D_{k}^{O,m,n}\right|\right)\|\phi_{k}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}\sum_{l=1}^{e(n)}\frac{\left|C_{l}^{A,m,n}-\tilde{C}_{l}^{A,m,n}\right|+\left|C_{l}^{O,m,n}-\tilde{C}_{l}^{O,m,n}\right|}{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{p=1}^{n}W_{l}^{m,n}}M

\displaystyle=M(1+\gamma)\sum_{k=1}^{e(n)}\left(\left|D_{k}^{A,m,n}\right|+\left|D_{k}^{O,m,n}\right|\right)\sum_{l=1}^{e(n)}\frac{\left|C_{l}^{A,m,n}-\tilde{C}_{l}^{A,m,n}\right|+\left|C_{l}^{O,m,n}-\tilde{C}_{l}^{O,m,n}\right|}{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{p=1}^{n}W_{l}^{m,n}}.

(A.68)

As

\displaystyle\|P_{\Pi_{n}}(X^{*,m},.)\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}\leq\sqrt{T}d_{n}+\|X^{*,m}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}.

We now note that

\displaystyle\left|C_{l}^{*,m,n}-\tilde{C}_{l}^{*,m,n}\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\int P_{\Pi_{n}}(X^{*,m},t)\left(T_{M}\left(\hat{\psi}_{l,n}(t)\right)-\psi_{l}(t)\right)dt\right|

\displaystyle\leq\|P_{\Pi_{n}}(X^{*,m},.)\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}\|T_{M}\left(\hat{\psi}_{l,n}\right)-\psi_{l}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\sqrt{T}d_{n}+\|X^{*,m}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}\right)\|T_{M}\left(\hat{\psi}_{l,n}\right)-\psi_{l}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}

(A.69)

Due to independence of the eigenfunction estimators from
\mathcal{F}_{n}

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\hat{B}_{n,(6)}-\hat{B}_{n,(5)}\|1_{A_{n,1}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\ \|B^{(m),6}-B^{(m),5}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}1_{A_{n,1}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq(1+\gamma)\frac{M}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}\sum_{k=1}^{e(n)}\sum_{l=1}^{e(n)}\sqrt{\frac{{\mathbb{E}}\left[Q_{l}\right]}{R_{l,n}}}

\displaystyle\times{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(\left|D_{k}^{A,m,n}\right|+\left|D_{k}^{O,m,n}\right|\right)\left(\sqrt{T}d_{n}+\|X^{A,m}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}+\|X^{O,m}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}\right)\|T_{M}\left(\hat{\psi}_{l,n}\right)-\psi_{l}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}1_{A_{n,1}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2(1+\gamma)\frac{M}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}\sum_{k=1}^{e(n)}\sum_{l=1}^{e(n)}\sqrt{\frac{{\mathbb{E}}\left[Q_{l}\right]}{R_{l,n}}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|D_{k}^{A,m,n}\right|^{2}+\left|D_{k}^{O,m,n}\right|^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\times{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(\sqrt{T}d_{n}+\|X^{A}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}+\|X^{O}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}\right)^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|T_{M}\left(\hat{\psi}_{l,n}\right)-\psi_{l}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq 2(1+\gamma)M\left(Td_{n}^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|Y^{A}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}^{2}+\|Y^{O}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}^{2}\right]\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(2^{2}\left(T^{2}d_{n}^{2}+T{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|X^{A}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}^{2}+\|X^{O}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}^{2}\right]\right)\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\times e(n)\sum_{l=1}^{e(n)}\sqrt{\frac{{\mathbb{E}}\left[Q_{l}\right]}{R_{l,n}}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|T_{M}\left(\hat{\psi}_{l,n}\right)-\psi_{l}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}

which converges to zero if
e(n)\leq E_{6}(n)
. Therefore, again by the Markov inequality
\|\hat{B}_{n,(4)}-\hat{B}_{n,(5)}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})}\xrightarrow{{\mathbb{P}}}0
.

Step 7: Replace the denominators using the estimated eigenfunctions.

Let

\tilde{C}_{l}^{\prime A,m,n}=\langle P_{\Pi_{n}^{\prime}}(X^{\prime A,m},.)\hat{\psi}_{l}\rangle_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p}},

\tilde{C}_{l}^{\prime O,m,n}=\langle P_{\Pi_{n}^{\prime}}(X^{\prime O,m},.)\hat{\psi}_{l}\rangle_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])^{p}},

\tilde{W}_{l}^{m,n}=\gamma(\tilde{C}_{l}^{\prime A,m,n})^{2}+(1-\gamma)(\tilde{C}_{l}^{\prime O,m,n})^{2}

B^{(m),7}=\sum_{k=1}^{e(n)}\sum_{l=1}^{e(n)}\frac{\gamma\tilde{C}_{l}^{A,m,n}D_{k}^{A,m,n}+(1-\gamma)\tilde{C}_{l}^{O,m,n}D_{k}^{O,m,n}\tilde{C}_{l}^{m,n}D_{k}^{m,n}}{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}\tilde{W}_{l}^{m,n}}\phi_{k}\otimes T_{M}\left(\hat{\psi}_{l,n}\right)

and
\hat{B}_{n,(7)}=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}B^{(m),7}(t,\tau)
. Similarly to (
A.8
),

|\tilde{C}^{\prime*,m,n}_{l}|\geq|C^{\prime*,m,n}_{l}|-\left(\sqrt{T}d_{n}+\|X^{*,m}\|_{L^{2}([0,T])}\right)\|T_{M}\left(\hat{\psi}_{l,n}\right)-\psi_{l}\|_{L^{2}([0,T])},

which is positive for all
l
, for sufficiently large
n
. Using that
a^{2}\geq b^{2}-2bc
if
a\geq b-c\geq 0
,
a,b,c\geq 0
and that the second term on the right-hand side above tends to zero, we may therefore define
E_{7}(n)
such that for
1\leq l\leq E_{7}(n)
,

{\mathbb{E}}\left[|\tilde{C}^{\prime m,n}_{l}|^{2}\right]\geq{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{|C^{\prime m,n}_{l}|^{2}}{2}\right]
(note that this inequality is valid across all
m
because of identical distribution). Next we note that,

\displaystyle\left|W_{l}^{m,n}-\tilde{W}_{l}^{m,n}\right|\leq(1+\gamma)\left(\left|C_{l}^{\prime A,m,n}-\tilde{C}_{l}^{\prime A,m,n}\right|+\left|C_{l}^{\prime O,m,n}-\tilde{C}_{l}^{\prime O,m,n}\right|\right)\left(\left|\tilde{C}_{l}^{\prime A,m,n}\right|+\left|C_{l}^{\prime A,m,n}\right|+\left|\tilde{C}_{l}^{\prime O,m,n}\right|+\left|C_{l}^{\prime O,m,n}\right|\right)

implying that

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{v=1}^{n}\tilde{W}_{l}^{v,n}-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{v=1}^{n}W_{l}^{v,n}\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2^{6}(1+\gamma){\mathbb{E}}\left[\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{v=1}^{n}\left|C_{l}^{\prime A,v,n}-\tilde{C}_{l}^{\prime A,v,n}\right|^{2}+\left|C_{l}^{\prime O,v,n}-\tilde{C}_{l}^{\prime O,v,n}\right|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\times\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{v=1}^{n}\left|\tilde{C}_{l}^{\prime A,v,n}\right|^{2}+\left|C_{l}^{\prime A,v,n}\right|^{2}+\left|\tilde{C}_{l}^{\prime O,v,n}\right|^{2}+\left|C_{l}^{\prime O,v,n}\right|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2^{6}(1+\gamma){\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{v=1}^{n}\left|C_{l}^{\prime A,v,n}-\tilde{C}_{l}^{\prime A,v,n}\right|^{2}+\left|C_{l}^{\prime O,v,n}-\tilde{C}_{l}^{\prime O,v,n}\right|^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\times{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{v=1}^{n}\left|\tilde{C}_{l}^{\prime A,v,n}\right|^{2}+\left|C_{l}^{\prime A,v,n}\right|^{2}+\left|\tilde{C}_{l}^{\prime O,v,n}\right|^{2}+\left|C_{l}^{\prime O,v,n}\right|^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle=2^{6}(1+\gamma){\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|C_{l}^{\prime A,1,n}-\tilde{C}_{l}^{\prime A,1,n}\right|^{2}+\left|C_{l}^{\prime O,1,n}-\tilde{C}_{l}^{\prime O,1,n}\right|^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\times{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|\tilde{C}_{l}^{\prime A,1,n}\right|^{2}+\left|C_{l}^{\prime A,1,n}\right|^{2}+\left|\tilde{C}_{l}^{\prime O,1,n}\right|^{2}+\left|C_{l}^{\prime O,1,n}\right|^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Similarly to (
A.8
) and due to the independence of the eigenfunctions estimators,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|C_{l}^{\prime*,m,n}-\tilde{C}_{l}^{\prime*,m,n}\right|^{2}\right]\leq 2\left(Td_{n}^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|X^{*}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}^{2}\right]\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\|T_{M}\left(\hat{\psi}_{l,n}\right)-\psi_{l}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}^{2}\right].

(A.70)

Therefore

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{v=1}^{n}\tilde{W}_{l}^{v,n}-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{v=1}^{n}W_{l}^{v,n}\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq 4\left(Td_{n}^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|X^{A}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}^{2}+\|X^{O}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}^{2}\right]\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\|T_{M}\left(\hat{\psi}_{l,n}\right)-\psi_{l}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\times{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|\tilde{C}_{l}^{\prime A,1,n}\right|^{2}+\left|C_{l}^{\prime A,1,n}\right|^{2}+\left|\tilde{C}_{l}^{\prime O,1,n}\right|^{2}+\left|C_{l}^{\prime O,1,n}\right|^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}},

which converges to zero for every
l\in{\mathbb{N}}
. By the triangle inequality it the follows that

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{v=1}^{n}\tilde{W}_{l}^{v,n}-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{v=1}^{n}W_{l}^{v}\right|\right]

converges to zero as well.
Let

A_{n,2}=\left\{\frac{{\mathbb{E}}\left[Q_{l}\right]^{2}}{2}\leq\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{v=1}^{n}\tilde{W}_{l}^{v,n}\right)^{2}\leq 2{\mathbb{E}}\left[Q_{l}\right]^{2},l=1,..,e(n)\right\}\cap A_{n,1}.

We may now define
E_{7}(n)
such that if
e(n)\leq E_{7}(n)
then
\lim_{n\to\infty}{\mathbb{P}}\left(A_{n,2}^{c}\right)=0
.
We have the following bound

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|B^{(m),7}-B^{(m),6}\|_{L^{2}([0,T]^{2})}1_{A_{n,2}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq(1+\gamma){\mathbb{E}}\left[\sum_{k=1}^{e(n)}\sum_{l=1}^{e(n)}\left(\left|\tilde{C}_{l}^{A,m,n}\right|+\left|\tilde{C}_{l}^{O,m,n}\right|\right)\left|\frac{1}{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{p=1}^{n}\tilde{W}_{l}^{m,n}}-\frac{1}{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{p=1}^{n}W_{l}^{m,n}}\right|\right.

\displaystyle\left.\times\left(\left|D_{k}^{A,m,n}\right|+\left|D_{k}^{O,m,n}\right|\right)\left(\left|\tilde{C}_{l}^{A,m,n}\right|+\left|\tilde{C}_{l}^{O,m,n}\right|\right)\|\phi_{k}\otimes T_{M}\left(\hat{\psi}_{l,n}\right)\|_{L^{2}([0,T]^{2})}1_{A_{n,2}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq(1+\gamma)M{\mathbb{E}}\left[\sum_{k=1}^{e(n)}\sum_{l=1}^{e(n)}\left(\left|D_{k}^{A,m,n}\right|+\left|D_{k}^{O,m,n}\right|\right)\left(\left|\tilde{C}_{l}^{A,m,n}\right|+\left|\tilde{C}_{l}^{O,m,n}\right|\right)\|\phi_{k}\|_{L^{2}([0,T]^{2})}\right]

\displaystyle\times{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{p=1}^{n}\tilde{W}_{l}^{m,n}-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{p=1}^{n}W_{l}^{m,n}\right|}{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{p=1}^{n}W_{l}^{m,n}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{p=1}^{n}\tilde{W}_{l}^{m,n}}M1_{A_{n,2}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2(1+\gamma)M4\left(Td_{n}^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|X^{A}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}^{2}+\|X^{O}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}^{2}\right]\right)\sum_{k=1}^{e(n)}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|D_{k}^{A,m,n}\right|^{2}+\left|D_{k}^{O,m,n}\right|^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\times\sum_{l=1}^{e(n)}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|\tilde{C}_{l}^{A,m,n}\right|^{2}+\left|\tilde{C}_{l}^{O,m,n}\right|^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}\frac{{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|T_{M}\left(\hat{\psi}_{l,n}\right)-\psi_{l}\|_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}])}^{2}\right]}{\frac{1}{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[Q_{l}\right]^{2}}.

(A.71)

We can therefore define
E_{8}(n)
such that if
e(n)\leq E_{8}(n)
then
{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|B^{(m),7}-B^{(m),6}\|_{L^{2}([0,T]^{2})}1_{A_{n,2}}\right]\to 0
.
To summarize, using the triangle inequality together with steps 1-7 yields the final result.
∎

A.9
Proof of Theorem
4.3

Proof.

Step 1: Use the entire sample curves

By assumption there exists
N^{\prime}\in{\mathbb{N}}
such that if
n\geq N^{\prime}
then
G_{n}
is full rank.
Define

\hat{G}_{n,1}(M)=\sqrt{\gamma}\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=1}^{M}F_{1:n}\left(X^{A,m}\right)^{T}F_{1:n}\left(X^{A,m}\right)+(1-\sqrt{\gamma})\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=1}^{M}F_{1:n}\left(X^{O,m}\right)^{T}F_{1:n}\left(X^{O,m}\right)

and

\displaystyle\left(\hat{\lambda}_{1,k,1}^{(1)}(n,M),\ldots,\hat{\lambda}_{1,k,n}^{(1)}(n,M),\ldots,\hat{\lambda}_{p,k,n}^{(1)}(n,M)\right)

\displaystyle=\hat{G}_{n,1}(M)^{-1}\left(\gamma\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=1}^{M}Z_{k}^{A,m}F_{1:n}\left(X^{A,m}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.+(1-\gamma)\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=1}^{M}Z_{k}^{O,m}F_{1:n}\left(X^{O,m}\right)\right)1_{\det\left(\hat{G}_{n,1}(M)\right)\not=0},

for
1\leq k\leq n
and

\displaystyle\hat{\beta}_{n,1,M}=\left(\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\hat{\lambda}_{1,k,1}^{(1)}(n,M)\phi_{k}\otimes\phi_{l},\ldots,\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\hat{\lambda}_{p,k,n}^{(1)}(n,M)\phi_{k}\otimes\phi_{l}\right).

For notational convenience we also let

\lambda(n)=\left(\lambda_{1,1,1}(n),\ldots,\lambda_{p,n,n}(n)\right).

and

\hat{\lambda}(n,M)=\left(\hat{\lambda}_{1,1,1}(n,M),\ldots,\hat{\lambda}_{p,n,n}(n)\right).

By the law of large numbers and the continuity of the determinant (in terms of its entries) it follows that for sufficiently large
M
,
\det\left(\hat{G}_{n,1}(M)\right)\not=0
, for
n\geq N^{\prime}
. By continuity and the law of large numbers it then follows that
\lim_{M\to\infty}\hat{\lambda}(n,M)=\lambda(n)
, a.s. for every
n\in{\mathbb{N}}
. From this we may then readily conclude that
\lim_{M\to\infty}\lVert\hat{\beta}_{n,1,M}-\beta_{n}\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})^{p}}=0
, a.s. for every
n\geq N
.

Step 2: Discretize the ”numerators”.

C_{l}^{A,m,n}(i)=\int P_{\Pi_{n}}(X^{A,m}(i),t)\phi_{l}(t)dt,

C_{l}^{O,m,n}(i)=\int P_{\Pi_{n}}(X^{O,m}(i),t)\phi_{l}(t)dt,

C_{l}^{\prime A,m,n}(i)=\int P_{\Pi_{n}}(X^{\prime A,m}(i),t)\phi_{l}(t)dt,

C_{l}^{\prime O,m,n}(i)=\int P_{\Pi_{n}}(X^{\prime O,m}(i),t)\phi_{l}(t)dt,

D_{k}^{A,m,n}=\int P_{\Pi_{n}}(Y^{A,m},t)\phi_{k}(t)dt
and

D_{k}^{O,m,n}=\int P_{\Pi_{n}}(Y^{O,m},t)\phi_{k}(t)dt
.

Let

\displaystyle\hat{G}_{n,2}(M)

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\gamma}\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\left(C_{1}^{A,m,M}(1),\ldots,C_{n}^{A,m,M}(p)\right)^{T}\left(C_{1}^{A,m,M}(1),\ldots,C_{n}^{A,m,M}(p)\right)

\displaystyle+(1-\sqrt{\gamma})\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\left(C_{1}^{O,m,M}(1),\ldots,C_{n}^{O,m,M}(p)\right)^{T}\left(C_{1}^{O,m,M}(1),\ldots,C_{n}^{O,m,M}(p)\right),

\displaystyle\left(\hat{\lambda}_{1,k,1}^{(2)}(n,M),\ldots,\hat{\lambda}_{1,k,n}^{(2)}(n,M),\ldots,\hat{\lambda}_{p,k,n}^{(2)}(n,M)\right)

\displaystyle=\hat{G}_{n,2}(M)^{-1}\left(\gamma\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=1}^{M}D_{k}^{A,m,M}\left(C_{1}^{A,m,M}(1),\ldots,C_{n}^{A,m,M}(p)\right)+\right.

\displaystyle\left.(1-\gamma)\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=1}^{M}D_{k}^{O,m,M}\left(C_{1}^{O,m,M}(1),\ldots,C_{n}^{O,m,M}(p)\right)\right)1_{\det\left(\hat{G}_{n,2}(M)\right)\not=0},

for
1\leq k\leq n
and

\displaystyle\hat{\beta}_{n,2,M}=\left(\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\hat{\lambda}_{1,k,1}^{(2)}(n,M)\phi_{k}\otimes\phi_{l},\ldots,\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\hat{\lambda}_{p,k,n}^{(2)}(n,M)\phi_{k}\otimes\phi_{l}\right).

Due to the definitions of
C_{l}^{A,m,n}(i),C_{l}^{O,m,n}(i),D_{k}^{A,m,n}
and
D_{k}^{O,m,n}
, for every
n\in{\mathbb{N}}
we get that
\hat{G}_{n,2}(M)-\hat{G}_{n,1}(M)
converges to zero as
M\to\infty
, for
n\geq N
. In the final step of the proof we choose
\{e(n)\}_{n\in{\mathbb{N}}}\subseteq{\mathbb{N}}
such that
e(n)\to\infty
,
\lim_{n\to\infty}\lVert\hat{\beta}_{e(n),1,n}-\beta_{e(n)}\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})^{p}}=0
and
\lim_{n\to\infty}\lVert\hat{\beta}_{e(n),2,n}-\hat{\beta}_{e(n),1,n}\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})^{p}}=0
. Since
\mathsf{dist}\left(\beta_{n},S\right)=\inf_{s\in S}\lVert\beta_{n}-s\rVert_{L^{2}([T_{1},T_{2}]^{2})^{p}}\to 0
it follows that
\mathsf{dist}\left(\hat{\beta}_{e(n),2,n},S\right)\to 0
.
∎
